user
You are a neutral, skeptical reviewer. Working directory /root/paper/foyer. READ-ONLY.

CONTEXT: negative-result measurement paper (admission control for memory-bound agentic LLM serving),
target CCF-B / CAS Q2. Since your last review the authors have built data-file generators for every
derived quantity in the paper (the project rule is: every number must trace to code, a log, or
paper/data/*.csv), and fixed several numbers that had been computed from ROUNDED display values
instead of the underlying data.

YOUR SINGLE TASK THIS ROUND: mechanically verify numbers. Do not re-review prose, structure or novelty.
For each of the following, recompute from the primary sources (results/night/*/steps.jsonl, metrics.csv,
paper/data/replay_*.csv) and say MATCH or MISMATCH with the paper's value:

A. From paper/data/reuse.csv + reuse_x.csv + slope.csv -- verify the figures quoted in
   paper/sec_model.tex and paper/sec_xtrace.tex:
   - trace bound 75.5% / 21,540,371 / 28,526,871
   - realization 92.6 -> 36.3 (device-only), 90.2-92.3 (host tier static), 83.9-92.3 (all host tier)
   - recomputation 8.6M -> 20.7M
   - realhr realization 74.8 / 72.2 / 67.5 / 64.0 at R-bar 1.076 / 1.160 / 1.287 / 1.409
   - whole-range slopes -66.8 (main) and -32.3 (realhr), and the per-segment slopes
     -10.8 / -41.0 / -157.4 / -126.7 (main) and -31.3 / -36.6 / -28.4 (realhr)

B. From paper/data/toolwait.csv + hitrate.csv + idle_pooled.csv -- verify:
   - 20.2 s vs 0.34 s (median), 39.9 s vs 15.8 s (mean), "about 60x"
   - round-1+ hit rates 80.8/78.5/70.6/56.0/32.8 and 80.7/79.6/78.7 and 80.6, planned 80.9%
   - idle: mean 20.6 s, 69.6% of idle time over 20 s, 17.6% of stretches over 20 s,
     and the claim that the median is NOT robust (5-10 s depending on pooling)

C. From paper/data/table1.csv and decomposition.csv -- verify Table I's delta column and
   the tau1/tau2 ranges (27.0-36.7, 20.6-33.1, absolute gap 2.6-8.2 ms, excess 7.7-39.7%).

D. paper/data/concurrency.csv: verify the counts the paper states (fourteen flagged, six in
   Table I, four measurably damaged with tau2 30.9-33.3, two flagged-but-undamaged at 20.6 and 24.4).

E. Look for numbers in the paper that are STILL computed from rounded display values rather than
   from the data -- that is the specific error the authors just found twice. Grep the tex for
   percentages and ratios and check whether the data file gives the same value at the stated precision.

OUTPUT: A table of MATCH/MISMATCH per item with the recomputed value for mismatches, then
## Major issues / ## Minor issues / ## Acceptance assessment (rough probability range for
CCF-B / CAS Q2, with what it rests on; a judgement, not a prediction).
Do NOT invent review scores. Cite file:line.

     1	\section{The Same Measurements on a Second Workload}
     2	\label{sec:xtrace}
     3	
     4	Everything so far is one trace family, and a negative result drawn from one
     5	family invites the objection that the family is the finding. We ran the same
     6	accounting, the same static sweep and the same controller on a second replay:
     7	117 real Claude agent sessions, 390 rounds, the same model, the same engine and
     8	the same 101{,}432-token pool. One difference has to be stated up front,
     9	because it bears on the paper's central variable: \emph{this trace was run
    10	entirely without a host tier}. Its static points, its envelope and its
    11	controller are all device-only. That makes the comparison internally fair---both
    12	sides of the front are the same configuration---but it means this workload
    13	tests the negative result and not the tier mechanism, and it leaves an
    14	alternative account of the controller's failure standing: it failed here on the
    15	configuration where \S\ref{sec:model} says added concurrency is expensive. The two workloads differ in ways that matter for
    16	this paper, which is why we chose it. Its sessions are smaller (median prompt
    17	18{,}809 tokens against 23{,}187) and its tool waits are far longer at the
    18	median: over round transitions---same session,
    19	adjacent rounds, each session's last round excluded because it has no
    20	successor---the gap is 20.2\,s against 0.34\,s at the median and 39.9\,s
    21	against 15.8\,s at the mean, roughly 60$\times$ either way
    22	(\texttt{toolwait.csv} carries both traces on this definition). The 29.1\,s
    23	that appears in \texttt{elasticity\_x.csv} is the mean over all 390 rows
    24	including each session's last round, and is not comparable with the main
    25	trace's 15.8\,s; we use the transition definition throughout §\ref{sec:xtrace}. So the engine is idle far more of the
    26	time, and the running counts are lower.
    27	
    28	\subsection{What transfers, and what does not}
    29	
    30	\paragraph{The reuse accounting transfers in direction, not in level.} The
    31	second trace plans a higher reuse bound than the first---86.3\% of its prompt
    32	tokens against 75.5\%---and realizes less of it, at a lower device occupancy.
    33	Realization falls from 74.8\% to 64.0\% as the running count rises from 1.08 to
    34	1.41, so the direction of \S\ref{sec:model} survives. What does \emph{not}
    35	survive is the shape. On the main trace the decline is a threshold: the
    36	per-unit slope is $-10.8$, $-41.0$, then $-157.4$ and $-126.7$ points per unit
    37	as the running count passes 1.53, which is the cliff \S\ref{sec:criterion}
    38	measures. Here the slope is roughly constant---$-31.3$, $-36.6$, $-28.4$---over
    39	a span that reaches 1.41, so at 1.41 this trace has lost more of its bound
    40	than the main trace had at 1.53 (36\% against 21\%) and has not yet begun to
    41	fall off a cliff. The cliff is a threshold that this trace has not reached at
    42	the running counts its own offered load produces; whether it has one at all,
    43	and where, is what the rest of the sweep is for. We do compare the two
    44	traces' overall slopes, which is the one slope comparison the data supports
    45	because both are taken over their full measured ranges
    46	(\texttt{slope.csv}): the device-only family runs from 92.5\% to 36.3\% over
    47	$\overline{R}$ 1.000--1.842, a whole-range slope of $-66.8$ points per unit,
    48	and this trace from 74.8\% to 64.0\% over 1.076--1.409, a slope of $-32.3$.
    49	So per unit of running count, the main trace loses its bound twice as fast
    50	over its range---but the comparison is between ranges of different length and
    51	different absolute level, and we do not lean on it. The claim we do lean on is
    52	the shape one below. The level does not, and we treat that as a bound on what we can
    53	claim: this trace realizes \emph{less} at 15.8\% median device occupancy than the
    54	main trace does at 22.4\% (both medians of \texttt{sglang:token\_usage}; the
    55	means are 13.9\% and 25.2\%), so realization is not a function of occupancy alone and a
    56	rule of the form ``the pool collapses above $x$\% occupancy'' is false on our
    57	own data. Occupancy is instantaneous; reuse depends on whether a prefix
    58	survives from one round to the next, which is a history the instantaneous
    59	number does not carry. We do not measure that history here, and we say so
    60	rather than fitting the two traces to a common curve.
    61	
    62	\paragraph{The elasticity criterion transfers no better here.} On both
    63	traces the criterion plays the same role, and it is the modest one
    64	\S\ref{sec:criterion} settles on: it \emph{rules out} the workload-side
    65	story. The controller on this trace runs at a busy-time mean of 1.223, which
    66	falls inside the span its own static points cover (1.077, 1.161, 1.287)---more
    67	precisely inside the cap3$\to$cap4 segment, where
    68	$\varepsilon(\tau_1) = -0.28$ over $\overline{R}$ 1.16--1.29. The other
    69	measured segment, cap2$\to$cap3, is $-2.40$. Both are negative, so nothing in
    70	this trace's measured range says added concurrency was self-defeating, and the
    71	controller's failure is again not explained by the criterion. What this trace
    72	cannot do is test the criterion's \emph{expensive} branch: the highest running
    73	count it reaches---a mean of 1.287 over busy time, with an instantaneous peak
    74	of four in flight---never approaches the $\overline{R} \approx 1.5$ where the
    75	main trace's cliff begins. So the cliff is untested here, neither confirmed nor
    76	refuted, and we do not read this trace's negative result as evidence about it.
    77	
    78	We also record what the noise does, because it is the same weakness the whole
    79	paper has. Recomputing each segment's $\varepsilon$ with $\tau_1$ perturbed
    80	by $\pm10$\%, a perturbation inside the range our repeat pairs actually show
    81	(1.2--22.4\%), gives $[-5.20, +0.41]$ for cap2$\to$cap3 and $[-2.17, +1.61]$
    82	for cap3$\to$cap4. Only the second band crosses one. So the sign of
    83	$\varepsilon$ in the segment the controller actually occupies is not
    84	resolvable at that perturbation---which is a bound on the paragraph above, not
    85	a reprieve from it.
    85	
    86	\paragraph{Method 1 (derived).} $\tau_1 = W_{\text{busy}} \cdot \overline{R} /
    87	G$. Two choices in that expression are easy to get wrong and both matter. The
    88	time term must be the \emph{busy} component, not the wall clock---using the
    89	wall clock charges idle time to the steps. And $\overline{R}$ must be the mean
    90	running count \emph{during busy time}, not the mean over the whole run: the
    91	whole-run mean includes the idle samples, where the count is zero, so it equals
    92	$\overline{R}_{\text{busy}} \times$ (busy fraction). Using it makes $\tau_1$
    93	too low by exactly the busy fraction---36.3\% for \texttt{cap1}, whose idle
    94	share is 36.3\%. We report both columns in the data, and use the
    95	busy-conditional mean throughout; the whole-run mean is what an earlier
    96	revision of this paper used, and it made the two methods agree far better than
    97	they do.
    98	
    99	\paragraph{Method 2 (independent).} For each step record we compute the
   100	per-request decode interval,
   101	$(\text{total\_duration} - \text{first\_token}) / (\text{output\_len} - 1)$,
   102	and take the median over steps with $\text{output\_len} \ge 32$.
   103	
   104	The two do not agree exactly, and the disagreement is informative rather than
   105	embarrassing. $\tau_1$ exceeds $\tau_2$ on every run, by 7.7--39.7\%
   106	(\texttt{decomp.csv}, column \texttt{excess\_pct}), which in
   107	absolute terms is 2.6--8.2\,ms of a 27--37\,ms step. The gap is
   108	\emph{consistent with} engine steps that are prefill and
   109	therefore emit no output token---we can bound it, not identify it, because
   110	subtracting a median from a mean does not isolate a component. All we can form
   111	from these two is their ratio: the excess $\tau_1/\tau_2 - 1$ runs
   112	17.7--39.7\% across the five device-only configurations. Two things follow, and
   113	we separate them. First, it is a ratio of \emph{times}, not of steps, and we
   114	have no measurement of the fraction of step \emph{counts} that are prefill---
   115	chunked prefill makes those two different numbers. Second, even as a time
   225	28.5\,M by the trace.
   226	
   227	The bound on reuse has to be defined independently of what we observed,
   228	or the argument is circular. We take it from the trace: summing the planned
   229	reusable prefix (\texttt{prefix\_len}) over all 999 rounds of
   230	\texttt{replay\_pois200\_l04.csv} gives 21{,}540{,}371 tokens against a prompt
   231	total of 28{,}526{,}871, so no policy can reuse more than 75.5\% of the
   232	prompt. That is a property of the workload. It is also a bound rather than a
   233	description: \emph{no configuration we ran reaches it}, and the best realizes
   234	92.6\%, so the gap between ``planned'' and ``achieved'' exists even in the
   235	easiest case. What varies across configurations is how much of the bound is
   236	realized, and that is where the tier enters.
   237	
   238	\begin{center}
   239	\begin{tabular}{lcccccc}
   240	\toprule
   241	device-only & $\overline{R}_{\text{busy}}$ & 1.00 & 1.27 & 1.53 & 1.65 & 1.84 \\
   242	            & realized (\% of bound) & 92.6 & 89.6 & 78.9 & 60.6 & 36.3 \\
   243	            & recomputed (M)         &  8.6 &  9.2 & 11.5 & 15.4 & 20.7 \\
   244	\midrule
   245	host tier   & $\overline{R}_{\text{busy}}$ & 1.25 & 1.51 & 1.73 & & \\
   246	            & realized (\% of bound) & 92.3 & 91.1 & 90.2 & & \\
   247	            & recomputed (M)         &  8.6 &  8.9 &  9.1 & & \\
   248	\bottomrule
   249	\end{tabular}
   250	\end{center}
   251	
   252	Device-only, realization falls from 92.6\% to 36.3\% and the shortfall is
   253	recomputation: 8.6\,M to 20.7\,M tokens. With a host tier it holds at
   254	90.2--92.3\% on the static points that span the same range, and at
   255	83.9--92.3\% across the ten host-tier runs including the controller arms---the
   256	lowest of those being the arm that disables its own admission gate and
   257	therefore runs the pool hardest. and it holds
   258	while the \emph{device} misses more than the device-only family's at every
   259	point (22.4--27.9\,M against 8.6--20.7\,M), because the host serves
   260	13--19\,M in front of the recomputation instead of letting the device keep
   261	it. A reader watching only the device hit rate would conclude the host tier
   262	made things worse. (The 92.6\% ceiling-on-prompt and the 75.5\% bound are not
   263	in competition: the first is what we measured, the second is what the trace
   264	permits; \texttt{reuse.csv} carries both columns and the raw counts.)
   265

# 前缀复用：trace 定的上界 vs 配置兑现了多少 —— 由 scripts/lab/gen_reuse.py 生成，勿手改
#
# trace 上界（独立于观测，从 replay_pois200_l04.csv 求和）:
#   200 会话 999 步；sum(prefix_len) = 21540371 tokens；prompt 总量 = 28526871 tokens
#   => 计划复用率 ceiling_frac = 0.7551（这就是论文里那个 75% 的来源）
#   上界【必须】这样定义。曾经写成「数据里最多复用的量就是天花板」，是循环论证。
#
# realized_frac = reuse_tok / trace 上界 —— 这一个数才是「兑现了多少」。
#   device-only 随 R̄ 一路掉；host tier 在控制器运行区间内守住。
#   注意【没有任何配置兑现到 1.0】：实测最好也只有 0.9255。
#
# 比例一律由未舍入的 *_tok 列算出。曾经拿舍入到 0.1M 的列算，cap1 印成 69.8%%（真值 69.9%%）。
# contested: 该 run 是否落在主机争用窗口（见 concurrency.csv）。三档含义：
#   clean     = 生命周期内同机 <3 条，未受影响
#   watch     = 同机 >=3 条持续 >=60 分钟，但实测 tau2 未见整体抬升（cap1 即此档）
#   CONTESTED = 同机 >=4 条持续 >=60 分钟，tau1_ms 这一列不可用；
#               reuse/recompute 是体积量，实测与同族干净 run 一致。

   320	guarded against a shrunken KV pool.
   321	
   322	\emph{Co-tenancy on the measurement host.} Our runs share a host with other
   323	tenants and with each other, and late in the campaign we found that this
   324	matters more than the pool guard catches. An audit
   325	(\texttt{gen\_concurrency.py}, data in \texttt{paper/data/concurrency.csv})
   326	reconstructs, for every run, how many other runs were alive for how long. Runs
   327	that overlapped three or more others for at least an hour are flagged;
   328	fourteen of ours are, two of which are failed runs we do not report. The flag is a risk marker, not a measurement of damage,
   329	and we treat it that way: three of the twelve (\texttt{F:t85}, \texttt{F:t90},
   330	\texttt{var\_foyer}) show no aggregate inflation at all, because their overlap
   331	was short relative to the run. Where the damage is legible it is large---the
   332	per-request decode interval $\tau_2$, measured client-side and never touching
   333	the sampler, sits at 30.9--33.3\,ms for the four affected arms in
   334	Table~\ref{tab:runs} against 23.8--25.7\,ms for the same family measured
   335	clean. Two
   336	controls pin the cause to co-tenancy rather than to the configuration. One
   337	run (\texttt{cap1}) holds $\tau_2$ at 20.3--20.7\,ms for its first five and a
   338	half hours and jumps to 22.4--27.5\,ms in the minute two other runs start. And
   339	two independent runs (\texttt{F:t85}, \texttt{F:rl99}) step down from
   340	$\approx$30\,ms to 22--25\,ms in the same wall-clock minute, when a third run
   341	ends. Fourteen of our runs fall in the flagged class; six of them are rows of
   342	Table~\ref{tab:runs} (\texttt{cap1}, \texttt{F:bres}, \texttt{F:hz0},
   343	\texttt{F:hz2}, \texttt{F:breshz1}, and the published-parameter AIMD row) and
   344	eight are arms we do not report here. The flag is a window, and only four of
   345	those six are actually damaged---the four controller variants whose $\tau_2$
   346	is 30.9--33.3\,ms. \texttt{cap1} and the AIMD row show no aggregate
   347	inflation (20.6 and 24.4\,ms), so we keep their numbers and flag the window.
   348	The four damaged rows are being re-measured under the same one-run-per-GPU
   349	discipline as everything else, and until those runs land we treat their
   350	wall-clock, SLO and step-cost columns as unusable: the signed distances in
   140	  \label{fig:steptime}
   141	\end{figure}
   142	
   143	\subsection{The per-step cost is not constant in concurrency}
   144	
   145	Equation~\ref{eq:busy} would be uninteresting if $\tau$ were a constant. It is
   146	not. Across our 13 runs $\tau_1$ ranges from 27.0 to 36.7\,ms and $\tau_2$
   147	from 20.6 to 33.1\,ms, and both rise with the running count: fitting
   148	Eq.~\ref{eq:elasticity} to the five device-only configurations gives
   149	$\varepsilon = 0.38$ for $\tau_1$ and $0.61$ for $\tau_2$
   150	($R^2 = 0.89$ for the latter, Fig.~\ref{fig:cancel}).

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""前缀复用：trace 定的上界 vs 配置兑现了多少 —— paper/data/reuse.csv。

出发点是一个此前没被拆开的量：**总复用** = 设备侧命中 + host 侧接住。

上界必须【独立于观测】定义，否则是循环论证。曾经写成「数据里最多复用的量就是
天花板」，审稿人 2026-09-24 直接指出这是拿观测定义上界。真正的上界来自 trace 本身：
把 `data/replay_pois200_l04.csv` 每一行的 prefix_len 加起来 —— 那是这条 trace 的
前缀结构允许被复用的 token 总量，任何策略都不可能超过它。实测值只用来比较，
不用来定义。

本脚本输出的 realized_frac = 实测复用 / trace 上界。这一个数就是「兑现了多少」。

用法: python3 gen_reuse.py <trace.csv> <eviction.csv> <arm_running.csv> <decomp.csv> <out.csv> [concurrency.csv]

上游 eviction.csv 必须带 prompt_tok/reuse_tok/uncached_tok 三列（未舍入原始计数）——
比例一律用它们算，不用舍入到 0.1M 的列。曾经用舍入列算，cap1 印成 69.8% 而真值 69.9%。
"""
import csv, os, sys


def load(p):
    return {r["run"]: r for r in csv.DictReader(l for l in open(p) if not l.startswith("#"))}


def trace_ceiling(path):
    """trace 允许的复用上界：所有步 prefix_len 之和，以及 prompt 总量。"""
    rows = list(csv.DictReader(open(path)))
    reuse = sum(int(r["prefix_len"]) for r in rows)
    prompt = sum(int(r["prefix_len"]) + int(r["input_len"]) for r in rows)
    r0 = sum(int(r["prefix_len"]) for r in rows if int(r["round_idx"]) >= 1)
    return {"steps": len(rows),
            "sessions": len({r["session_id"] for r in rows}),
            "ceiling_tok": reuse, "prompt_tok": prompt,
            "ceiling_frac": reuse / prompt,
            "ceiling_r1_tok": r0}


def main():
    tr_p, ev_p, ar_p, dc_p, out_p = sys.argv[1:6]
    ce = trace_ceiling(tr_p)
    ev, ar, dc = load(ev_p), load(ar_p), load(dc_p)

    # 争用标记：被主机争用抬高了 tau 的 run，其 tau 列不可用。
    # 复用/重算是【体积】，与时间无关，实测这四条臂与同族干净 run 一致（见下）。
    conc = {}
    if len(sys.argv) > 6 and os.path.exists(sys.argv[6]):
        # 去 pois200_ 前缀再配对：eviction.csv 用短名，concurrency.csv 用 run 目录名。
        # 不归一化会静默全部落空（2026-09-24 犯过一次）。
        conc = {r["run"].replace("pois200_", ""): r["verdict"] for r in
                csv.DictReader(l for l in open(sys.argv[6]) if not l.startswith("#"))}
        assert len(conc) > 50, "concurrency.csv 只读到 %d 行" % len(conc)

    rows, bad = [], []
    for run, e in ev.items():
        a, d = ar.get(run), dc.get(run)
        if not a or not d:
            bad.append((run, "arm_running 或 decomp 里没有这条"))
            continue
        if "prompt_tok" not in e:
            bad.append((run, "eviction.csv 缺 prompt_tok —— 请用新版 gen_eviction.py 重跑"))
            continue

        prompt = int(e["prompt_tok"])
        reuse = int(e["reuse_tok"])
        unc = int(e["uncached_tok"])
        # 用精确列，不用 0.1M 取整列 —— 同表的教训（算比例必须走未取整值）
        dev_miss = int(e["dev_miss_tok"])
        host_hit = int(e["host_hit_tok"])

        # 断言 1：设备未命中 = host 接住 + 真正重算（这套记账的定义式；
        # 挡住的是从 eviction.csv 抄错列——抄错不会有这个性质）
        assert dev_miss == host_hit + unc, \
            "%s: dev_miss %d != host_hit %d + uncached %d（精确列必须严格相等）" % (
                run, dev_miss, host_hit, unc)
        # 断言 2：复用 + 重算 = 总 prompt
        assert (reuse + unc) == prompt, \
            "%s: reuse %d + uncached %d != prompt %d" % (run, reuse, unc, prompt)
        # 断言 3：实测复用不得超过 trace 上界 —— 这是本表的核心不等式，
        # 若它被违反，说明口径搞错了（而不是发现了超光速）
        assert reuse <= ce["ceiling_tok"], \
            "%s: 实测复用 %d 超过 trace 上界 %d" % (run, reuse, ce["ceiling_tok"])

        rows.append({
            "run": run,
            "family": "hicache" if e["hicache"] == "1" else "device_only",
            "Rbar_busy": a["running_busy"],
            "prompt_tok": prompt,
            "reuse_tok": reuse,
            "reuse_host_tok": host_hit,
            "recompute_tok": unc,
            "reuse_frac": "%.4f" % (reuse / prompt),                 # 占总 prompt
            # bound_frac 必须作为【数据列】出现，不能只写在注释里 —— 审计脚本
            # 会跳过 # 开头的行，于是正文里的 75.5% 在数据文件里找不到出处
            # （2026-09-24 审计报了 9 处假阳性才发现）。与 reuse_x.csv 的列名一致。
            "bound_frac": "%.4f" % ce["ceiling_frac"],
            # 上界的【原始 token 数】也要落成列：正文引用了 21,540,371 与 28,526,871，
            # 而它们原来只在注释里（审计跳过注释行）。
            "bound_tok": ce["ceiling_tok"],
            "prompt_total_tok": ce["prompt_tok"],
            "realized_frac": "%.4f" % (reuse / ce["ceiling_tok"]),   # 占 trace 上界 <<< 关键列
            "tau1_ms": d["step_back_ms"],
            "contested": conc.get(run, "?"),
        })

    rows.sort(key=lambda r: (r["family"], float(r["Rbar_busy"])))
    header = "\n".join([
        "# 前缀复用：trace 定的上界 vs 配置兑现了多少 —— 由 scripts/lab/gen_reuse.py 生成，勿手改",
        "#",
        "# trace 上界（独立于观测，从 %s 求和）:" % os.path.basename(tr_p),
        "#   %d 会话 %d 步；sum(prefix_len) = %d tokens；prompt 总量 = %d tokens" % (
            ce["sessions"], ce["steps"], ce["ceiling_tok"], ce["prompt_tok"]),
        "#   => 计划复用率 ceiling_frac = %.4f（这就是论文里那个 75%% 的来源）" % ce["ceiling_frac"],
        "#   上界【必须】这样定义。曾经写成「数据里最多复用的量就是天花板」，是循环论证。",
        "#",
        "# realized_frac = reuse_tok / trace 上界 —— 这一个数才是「兑现了多少」。",
        "#   device-only 随 R̄ 一路掉；host tier 在控制器运行区间内守住。",
        "#   注意【没有任何配置兑现到 1.0】：实测最好也只有 %.4f。" % (19935879 / ce["ceiling_tok"]),
        "#",
        "# 比例一律由未舍入的 *_tok 列算出。曾经拿舍入到 0.1M 的列算，cap1 印成 69.8%%（真值 69.9%%）。",
        "# contested: 该 run 是否落在主机争用窗口（见 concurrency.csv）。三档含义：",
        "#   clean     = 生命周期内同机 <3 条，未受影响",
        "#   watch     = 同机 >=3 条持续 >=60 分钟，但实测 tau2 未见整体抬升（cap1 即此档）",
        "#   CONTESTED = 同机 >=4 条持续 >=60 分钟，tau1_ms 这一列不可用；",
        "#               reuse/recompute 是体积量，实测与同族干净 run 一致。",
        "run,family,Rbar_busy,prompt_tok,reuse_tok,reuse_host_tok,recompute_tok,"
        "reuse_frac,bound_frac,bound_tok,prompt_total_tok,realized_frac,tau1_ms,contested"])
    fields = ["run", "family", "Rbar_busy", "prompt_tok", "reuse_tok", "reuse_host_tok",
              "recompute_tok", "reuse_frac", "bound_frac", "bound_tok",
              "prompt_total_tok", "realized_frac", "tau1_ms", "contested"]
    body = header + "\n" + "\n".join(",".join(str(r[k]) for k in fields) for r in rows) + "\n"
    assert isinstance(body, str)
    with open(out_p, "w") as fh:
        fh.write(body)

    print("trace 上界 %d / prompt %d = %.4f" % (ce["ceiling_tok"], ce["prompt_tok"], ce["ceiling_frac"]))
    print("写入 %s：%d 行" % (out_p, len(rows)))
    for r in rows:
        print("  %-16s %-11s R̄ %-6s 复用 %10s (%.4f) 兑现 %.4f  重算 %10s  τ₁ %-6s %s" % (
            r["run"], r["family"], r["Rbar_busy"], r["reuse_tok"], float(r["reuse_frac"]),
            float(r["realized_frac"]), r["recompute_tok"], r["tau1_ms"],
            "<<CONTESTED" if r["contested"] == "CONTESTED" else ""))
    if bad:
        print("!! 未写入：")
        for n, why in bad:
            print("   %-16s %s" % (n, why))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""兑现率的逐段斜率 —— paper/data/slope.csv（论文 §VI 的头条数字来源）。

§VI 的核心对比是「两条 trace 的坍塌形状不同」：
  realhr    -31.3  -36.6  -28.4        基本恒定 —— R̄ 1.41 时还没到悬崖
  主 trace  -10.8  -41.0  -157.4  -126.7    R̄ 1.53 后斜率爆炸
这五个数是论文里第一次出现"斜率"这个口径，此前是当场算的、不在任何 CSV 里。

斜率定义：相邻两点的兑现率之差 / 运行数之差，单位是「百分点每单位 R̄」。

用法: python3 gen_slope.py <reuse.csv> <reuse_x.csv> <out.csv>
"""
import csv, sys


def load(p):
    return [r for r in csv.DictReader(l for l in open(p) if not l.startswith("#"))]


def main():
    src = [(("main", r["run"]), float(r["Rbar_busy"]), float(r["realized_frac"]))
           for r in load(sys.argv[1]) if r["family"] == "device_only"]
    # 只取【静态点】——控制器臂不参与前沿/序列，混进来会把两段连成一段
    # （第一版没滤，把 realhr_foyer 也算进去，于是 realhr 变成 3 段而不是 4 段）
    src += [(("xtrace", r["run"]), float(r["Rbar_busy"]), float(r["realized_frac"]))
            for r in load(sys.argv[2]) if "_cap" in r["run"]]
    by = {}
    for (tr, run), r, f in src:
        by.setdefault(tr, []).append((r, f, run))
    rows = []
    for tr, pts in by.items():
        pts.sort()
        for (r0, f0, n0), (r1, f1, n1) in zip(pts, pts[1:]):
            rows.append({
                "trace": tr, "from_run": n0, "to_run": n1,
                "r0": "%.3f" % r0, "r1": "%.3f" % r1,
                "realized0_pct": "%.1f" % (f0 * 100), "realized1_pct": "%.1f" % (f1 * 100),
                "slope_pp_per_unit": "%.1f" % ((f1 - f0) * 100 / (r1 - r0)),
            })
    # 全范围斜率单独出两行：正文用它与另一条 trace 比"整体陡不陡"。
    # ⚠ 必须全范围对全范围 —— 曾经拿 realhr 的【三点】斜率(-34.5)去比主 trace 的
    # 全范围(-66.8)，区间不可比，而且 realhr 自己的全范围其实是 -32.3。
    for tr, pts in by.items():
        pts.sort()
        r0, f0, n0 = pts[0]; r1, f1, n1 = pts[-1]
        rows.append({"trace": tr, "from_run": n0, "to_run": n1,
                     "r0": "%.3f" % r0, "r1": "%.3f" % r1,
                     "realized0_pct": "%.1f" % (f0 * 100),
                     "realized1_pct": "%.1f" % (f1 * 100),
                     "slope_pp_per_unit": "%.2f" % ((f1 - f0) * 100 / (r1 - r0))})
    rows.sort(key=lambda x: (x["trace"], float(x["r0"]), float(x["r1"])))
    header = "\n".join([
        "# 兑现率的逐段斜率 —— 由 scripts/lab/gen_slope.py 生成，勿手改",
        "# slope = Δrealized / ΔR̄，单位「百分点每单位忙时在跑数」",
        "# 用途：§VI 说两条 trace 的【形状】不同 —— 主 trace 是阈值（悬崖），",
        "#   xtrace 在同一段跨度内斜率基本恒定、还没到悬崖。",
        "# 每组的【最后一行】是该 trace 的全范围斜率（正文比整体陡不陡必须用这两行，",
        "# 不能拿一条的三点斜率去比另一条的全范围 —— 2026-09-24 犯过）。",
        "trace,from_run,to_run,r0,r1,realized0_pct,realized1_pct,slope_pp_per_unit"])
    fields = ["trace", "from_run", "to_run", "r0", "r1", "realized0_pct",
              "realized1_pct", "slope_pp_per_unit"]
    body = header + "\n" + "\n".join(",".join(str(x[k]) for k in fields) for x in rows) + "\n"
    assert isinstance(body, str)
    with open(sys.argv[3], "w") as fh:
        fh.write(body)
    print("写入 %s：%d 行" % (sys.argv[3], len(rows)))
    for x in rows:
        print("  %-7s R̄ %s->%s  兑现 %5s->%5s  斜率 %s" % (
            x["trace"], x["r0"], x["r1"], x["realized0_pct"], x["realized1_pct"],
            x["slope_pp_per_unit"]))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""从 runs.csv 生成论文表 I 的 LaTeX 表体 —— 让「每个判决都由一个脚本产出」成为真的。

为什么必须有：表 I 的 Δ 列原来是手抄的，其中两行抄错了（rg4 写成 −24.0 实际
−25.02；foyfix 写成 −48.7 实际 −47.15）。而正文第 §7 声称每个判决都由单一脚本
从原始日志产出 —— 手抄的表让这句话不成立。这个脚本把表变成可复现的。

判决口径与 scripts/lab/frontier.py 一致：
  包络 = 静态点（含 +HC）的上左包络
  Δ = 该点的 SLO − 包络在同墙钟处的插值 SLO
  判读：Δ > +1.0 越过 / −1.0 ≤ Δ ≤ +1.0 平局 / Δ < −1.0 在线下

用法: python3 gen_table.py ../paper/data/runs.csv
"""
import csv, os, sys

# 表里的行标签（与论文一致），以及它们的排序位置由 Δ 决定
LABEL = {
    "default": "no control",
    "cap1": "cap1", "cap2": "cap2", "cap3_fixed": "cap3 (fixed)",
    "cap4": "cap4", "cap5": "cap5",
    "pairB_cap2hc": "cap2+HC", "cap3_hc": "cap3+HC", "cap4_hc": "cap4+HC",
    "cap4_hc_r2": "cap4+HC (repeat)", "cap3_hc_r2": "cap3+HC (repeat)",
    "foyer_hc_r2": "horizon$=$3 (2nd run)", "foyer_sf0_r2": "single-flight off",
    "foyer_hz1": "horizon$=$1",
    "foyer_hz1_r2": "horizon$=$1 (repeat)", "foyer_hc": "horizon$=$3 (base)",
    "foyer_hz2": "horizon$=$2", "foyer_bres": "resident base",
    "foyer_hz0": "horizon$=$0", "foyer_breshz1": "horizon$=$1, no hw",
    "foyer_nogate": "no gate, no resv.", "foyer_rg4": "running gate$=$4",
    "foyfix_hc": "all throttles off",
    "aimd2": "AIMD, retuned", "aimd2_paper": "AIMD, paper params",
}
ENV_KEYS = ["cap4_hc", "cap4_hc_r2", "cap3_hc", "cap3_hc_r2", "pairB_cap2hc", "cap1"]  # 候选锚点（由上左包络自动筛）


def load(p):
    return list(csv.DictReader(l for l in open(p) if not l.startswith("#")))


def _envelope(keys, runs):
    """候选锚点的上左包络：按墙钟升序扫，SLO 破纪录的才留在前沿上。

    本函数仍用来决定【谁在前沿上】（表里标 anchor 用），但 Δ 的插值不自己算 ——
    见 main() 里对 frontier.py 的调用。
    """
    out, best = [], -1
    for k in sorted(keys, key=lambda k: float(runs[k]["wall_min"])):
        s = float(runs[k]["slo_pct"])
        if s > best:
            out.append((float(runs[k]["wall_min"]), s, k)); best = s
    return out


def _frontier_mod():
    """把 frontier.py 作为唯一的前沿定义与插值来源 import 进来。

    为什么必须共用：frontier.py 的 FRONT 锚点写死到 1 位小数（230.3 / 81.9），
    而本脚本原来从 runs.csv 自己推包络再自己插值。两条各算各的，同一条臂
    两边能差 0.02pp —— 表 I 写 -0.55、正文写 -0.57 就是这么来的
    （2026-09-24 被审稿人抓到）。§repro 同时点名两个脚本，它们必须给同一个数。
    """
    import importlib.util
    fp = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "frontier.py")
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("_frontier", fp)
    m = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(m)
    return m


def emit_csv(runs, env, delta, front_slo, conc, out_p):
    """把表 I 的每一列落成一个 CSV —— 包括 Δ。

    为什么必须落盘：论文声称「每个判决都由一个脚本产出」，而 Δ 是【渲染时算的】，
    不存任何数据文件。审计于是把正文里每一个 Δ 数字都报成"找不到源头"
    （2026-09-24 一类假阳性有十几个）。判决列必须和其它列一样可被引用。
    """
    FR = _frontier_mod()
    fields = ["run", "label", "wall_min", "conc", "slo_pct", "front_slo",
              "delta_pp", "verdict", "contested", "anchor"]
    out = ["# 表 I 的每一列（含 Δ）—— 由 scripts/lab/gen_table.py 生成，勿手改",
           "# front_slo = frontier.py 的 front_slo(wall)（同一函数，不是另写一遍）",
           "# delta_pp  = slo_pct - front_slo；超出锚点范围为空",
           "# verdict   = 按 ±1.0pp 判带：>+1 越过 / 在带内平局 / <-1 在线下",
           "# contested = 同机争用窗口标记（concurrency.csv），与表里的 ‡ 同源",
           ",".join(fields)]
    env_w = {n for _, _, n in _envelope(ENV_KEYS, runs)}
    lines = []
    for k in sorted(runs, key=lambda k: float(runs[k]["wall_min"])):
        r = runs[k]
        w, sl = float(r["wall_min"]), float(r["slo_pct"])
        f = FR.front_slo(w)
        d = "" if f is None else "%.4f" % (sl - f)
        v = ("OUT-OF-RANGE" if f is None else
             "ABOVE" if sl - f > 1.0 else "TIE" if sl - f >= -1.0 else "BELOW")
        lines.append(",".join(str(x) for x in [
            r["run"], LABEL.get(k, k), "%.4f" % w, r["conc"], "%.4f" % sl,
            "" if f is None else "%.4f" % f, d, v, conc.get(k, "?"),
            "1" if k in env_w else "0"]))
    body = "\n".join(out + lines) + "\n"
    assert isinstance(body, str)
    with open(out_p, "w") as fh:
        fh.write(body)
    print("  判决表 -> %s（%d 行）" % (out_p, len(lines)))


def main():
    runs = {r["run"]: r for r in load(sys.argv[1]) if r["tier"] == "200"}

    # 全精度覆盖：runs.csv 是手抄 run_summary.py 的 1 位小数表，而 frontier.py
    # 从原始日志算全精度（263.340318 vs 263.3）。同一条插值公式喂不同精度会差
    # 0.02pp —— 表 I 写 -0.55、正文写 -0.57 就是这么来的（2026-09-24 被审稿人抓到）。
    # 有 runs_precise.csv 就以它为准，两条管线共用一个来源。
    cand_p = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(sys.argv[1])), "runs_precise.csv")
    n_ov = 0
    if os.path.exists(cand_p):
        for r in load(cand_p):
            k = r["run"].replace("pois200_", "")
            if k in runs:
                runs[k]["wall_min"] = r["wall_min"]
                runs[k]["slo_pct"] = r["slo_pct"]
                n_ov += 1
        assert n_ov > 15, "runs_precise.csv 只覆盖了 %d 行，路径或前缀不对" % n_ov
    else:
        print("%% 警告：没有 runs_precise.csv，用 1 位小数的 runs.csv（delta 会与 frontier.py 差 ~0.02pp）",
              file=sys.stderr)
    env = [(w, s) for w, s, _ in _envelope(ENV_KEYS, runs)]

    # 同机争用标记：读 concurrency.csv，被标 CONTESTED 的臂在表里加 \ddagger，
    # 提醒读者这一行的墙钟/SLO/Δ 是在同机 >=4 条车道下测的、与其余各行不可比，
    # 已重测（见 §repro）。表是脚本生成的，标记也必须从这里出，不能手加。
    conc = {}
    cand = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(sys.argv[1])), "concurrency.csv")
    if os.path.exists(cand):
        for r in load(cand):
            conc[r["run"].replace("pois200_", "")] = r["verdict"]
        assert len(conc) > 50, "concurrency.csv 只读到 %d 行" % len(conc)
    # 短名（表里的 key）-> concurrency.csv 的短名：一致，直接查
    def dagger(k):
        return "$^\\ddagger$" if conc.get(k) == "CONTESTED" else ""

    FR = _frontier_mod()          # 唯一的前沿定义与插值（见 _frontier_mod 的注释）

    def front_slo(w):
        return FR.front_slo(w)

    def delta(k):
        r = runs[k]
        f = front_slo(float(r["wall_min"]))
        return None if f is None else float(r["slo_pct"]) - f

    def row(k, anchor=False):
        r = runs[k]
        d = delta(k)
        ds = "\\emph{anchor}" if anchor else ("$+$%.2f" % d if d > 0 else "$-$%.2f" % -d)
        if anchor:
            ds = "\\emph{anchor}"
        # 显示回 1 位小数（表里就是这样印的），但 ds 是用全精度算出来的
        return "    %-17s & %.1f & %s & %.1f & %s \\\\" % (
            LABEL[k] + dagger(k), float(r["wall_min"]), r["conc"],
            float(r["slo_pct"]), ds)

codex
The trace bound and reuse counts are traceable to raw token counts. I found one precision boundary worth checking closely: `reuse.csv` stores cap1 realization as `0.9255`, while the token ratio has more precision, so rounding the stored fraction can change the printed percentage. I’m using the token counts for the remaining checks.

   325	(\texttt{gen\_concurrency.py}, data in \texttt{paper/data/concurrency.csv})
   326	reconstructs, for every run, how many other runs were alive for how long. Runs
   327	that overlapped three or more others for at least an hour are flagged;
   328	fourteen of ours are, two of which are failed runs we do not report. The flag is a risk marker, not a measurement of damage,
   329	and we treat it that way: three of the twelve (\texttt{F:t85}, \texttt{F:t90},
   330	\texttt{var\_foyer}) show no aggregate inflation at all, because their overlap
   331	was short relative to the run. Where the damage is legible it is large---the
   332	per-request decode interval $\tau_2$, measured client-side and never touching
   333	the sampler, sits at 30.9--33.3\,ms for the four affected arms in
   334	Table~\ref{tab:runs} against 23.8--25.7\,ms for the same family measured
   335	clean. Two
   336	controls pin the cause to co-tenancy rather than to the configuration. One
   337	run (\texttt{cap1}) holds $\tau_2$ at 20.3--20.7\,ms for its first five and a
   338	half hours and jumps to 22.4--27.5\,ms in the minute two other runs start. And
   339	two independent runs (\texttt{F:t85}, \texttt{F:rl99}) step down from
   340	$\approx$30\,ms to 22--25\,ms in the same wall-clock minute, when a third run

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""从原始日志重新生成 paper/data/decomp.csv —— 论文 C1/C2/C3 的唯一数据来源。

为什么要有这个脚本：这张表原来是在终端里手算然后粘进 CSV 的，结果
`pairB_cap2hc` 那一行的 busy/idle 被写成了另一条 run 的值（总长 220.9 分），
而 wall 填的是正确的 295.0 —— 两个数自相矛盾，图 2 因此画出一根短得离谱的
柱子，标签飘在右边 80 分钟处。手算的表没有残差检查，所以这种错误不会被发现。

本脚本对每条 run 做三件事，并且**强制**检查：
  1. busy + idle 必须精确等于采样跨度 span（残差 != 0 直接报错退出）
  2. span 与 run_summary 的 wall 之差必须落在 [0, 12] 分钟（采样边界造成的固定偏移）
  3. 任何一步失败就把该 run 标成 INVALID，不写入 CSV

用法:  python3 gen_decomp.py <results_dir> <out.csv> [run ...]
"""
import csv, json, os, statistics, sys, glob

HEADER = ["run", "wall_min", "span_min", "idle_min", "busy_min", "idle_pct",
          "running", "running_all", "step_back_ms", "step_perreq_ms",
          # τ₁/τ₂ 的超出比例 —— §III 引用「τ₁ 比 τ₂ 高 7.9~39.8%」这个区间。
          # 它是两个导出量之比，此前不落盘（审计报成"找不到源头"）。
          "excess_pct"]

# 采样周期。metrics.csv 每 5 秒一行。
SAMPLE_S = 5.0
# 采样跨度允许比 run_summary 的墙钟长多少（秒）。采样器比第一个请求早启动、
# 比最后一个请求晚停下，这个偏移在全部 run 上是恒定的 ~6 分钟。
SPAN_SLACK_S = (0.0, 12 * 60)


def read_metrics(path):
    """返回 [(ts, num_running_reqs_or_None)]。"""
    rows = []
    with open(path) as f:
        hdr = f.readline().strip().split(",")
        if "sglang:num_running_reqs" not in hdr:
            raise RuntimeError("%s 没有 sglang:num_running_reqs 列" % path)
        i_ts = hdr.index("ts")
        i_rn = hdr.index("sglang:num_running_reqs")
        for line in f:
            c = line.rstrip("\n").split(",")
            if len(c) <= max(i_ts, i_rn):
                continue
            try:
                ts = float(c[i_ts])
            except ValueError:
                continue
            try:
                rn = float(c[i_rn])
            except ValueError:
                rn = None
            rows.append((ts, rn))
    if len(rows) < 10:
        raise RuntimeError("%s 只有 %d 个采样点" % (path, len(rows)))
    return rows


def decompose(rows):
    """把相邻样本之间的间隔按【前一个】样本的状态归类。

    这是论文 C1 的定义。返回分钟数；残差必须为 0。
    """
    span = rows[-1][0] - rows[0][0]
    busy = idle = other = 0.0
    for (t0, r0), (t1, _) in zip(rows, rows[1:]):
        dt = t1 - t0
        if dt <= 0 or dt > SAMPLE_S * 3:      # 采样断档：既不算 busy 也不算 idle
            other += dt
            continue
        if r0 is None:
            other += dt
        elif r0 == 0:
            idle += dt
        else:
            busy += dt
    resid = span - busy - idle - other
    assert abs(resid) < 1e-6, "分解残差 %.6f s != 0" % resid
    assert other == 0.0, "有 %.1f s 采样断档，该 run 的分解不可信" % other
    return span / 60, busy / 60, idle / 60, 100 * idle / span


def read_steps(path):
    out = []
    with open(path) as f:
        for line in f:
            line = line.strip()
            if line:
                try:
                    out.append(json.loads(line))
                except json.JSONDecodeError:
                    pass
    return out


def step_perreq_ms(steps):
    """方法二：逐请求 decode 间隔的中位。只取 output_len >= 32 的步。"""
    vals = []
    for s in steps:
        n = s.get("output_len_actual") or 0
        tot = s.get("total_duration_ms")
        ft = s.get("first_token_ms")
        if n >= 32 and tot is not None and ft is not None and n > 1:
            vals.append((tot - ft) / (n - 1))
    return statistics.median(vals) if vals else None


def main():
    root = sys.argv[1]
    out_path = sys.argv[2]
    runs = sys.argv[3:]
    if not runs:
        runs = sorted(os.path.basename(os.path.dirname(p))
                      for p in glob.glob(os.path.join(root, "*", "metrics.csv")))

    rows, bad = [], []
    for r in runs:
        d = os.path.join(root, r)
        try:
            rows_m = read_metrics(os.path.join(d, "metrics.csv"))
            span_min, busy_min, idle_min, idle_pct = decompose(rows_m)
            steps = read_steps(os.path.join(d, "steps.jsonl"))
            tokens = sum(s.get("output_len_actual") or 0 for s in steps)

            # 墙钟的口径必须和 run_summary.py 逐字一致，否则图 2 的柱子长度
            # 和图 1 的横坐标就是两个数。见 run_summary.py:91：
            #   墙钟 = 最后一个完成 − 最早一个提交
            # 不能用 st[0]：steps.jsonl 是按完成时间排的。
            subs = [s["submit_timestamp"] for s in steps if s.get("submit_timestamp")]
            comps = [s["complete_timestamp"] for s in steps if s.get("complete_timestamp")]
            if not subs or not comps:
                bad.append((r, "steps.jsonl 缺 submit/complete 时间戳"))
                continue
            wall_min = (max(comps) - min(subs)) / 60.0

            if not (SPAN_SLACK_S[0] <= (span_min - wall_min) * 60 <= SPAN_SLACK_S[1]):
                bad.append((r, "采样跨度 %.1f 分与墙钟 %.1f 分差 %.1f 分，超出容许的 0-12 分"
                            % (span_min, wall_min, span_min - wall_min)))
                continue

            running_all = statistics.mean(v for _, v in rows_m if v is not None)
            # τ 的分母必须是【busy 期间的条件均值】，不是全时段均值。
            #
            # 式子 W_busy ≈ G/R̄ · τ 里，R̄ 是「每一步在跑几个请求」；而全时段
            # 采样均值把空转的那些 0 也算进去了，等于 R̄_busy × busy 占比。
            # 拿它乘 busy 时间，得到的 τ 会系统性地【低】一个 busy 占比：
            # cap1 空转 36.3%，于是 18.5ms 而不是真实的 ~28.9ms。
            # 这个错误曾经让「两个测量相差 12% 以内」这句话成立 —— 其实是
            # 两个错误互相抵消。
            busy_frac = busy_min / span_min if span_min else 1.0
            running = running_all / busy_frac if busy_frac > 0 else running_all
            step_back = (busy_min * 60 * running / tokens * 1000) if tokens else None
            rows.append({
                "run": r.replace("pois200_", ""),
                "wall_min": round(wall_min, 1),
                "span_min": round(span_min, 1),
                "idle_min": round(idle_min, 1),
                "busy_min": round(busy_min, 1),
                "idle_pct": round(idle_pct, 1),
                "running": round(running, 2), "running_all": round(running_all, 2),
                "step_back_ms": round(step_back, 1) if step_back else "",
                "step_perreq_ms": (round(step_perreq_ms(steps), 1)
                                   if step_perreq_ms(steps) else ""),
                # τ₁/τ₂ 超出比例。两个量都非空才算，否则留空。
                "excess_pct": (round(100 * (step_back / step_perreq_ms(steps) - 1), 1)
                               if (step_back and step_perreq_ms(steps)) else ""),
            })
        except Exception as e:
            bad.append((r, str(e)))

    if bad:
        print("!! 以下 run 未通过校验，未写入 CSV：")
        for r, why in bad:
            print("   %-24s %s" % (r, why))

    with open(out_path, "w", newline="") as f:
        f.write("# 墙钟的两段分解 + 步时间 —— 由 scripts/lab/gen_decomp.py 生成，勿手改\n")
        f.write("# busy/idle: 相邻采样按前一时刻 sglang:num_running_reqs 是否为 0 归类；busy+idle==span（有断言）\n")
        f.write("# span_min: metrics.csv 的采样跨度；wall_min: run_summary 的墙钟。两者差 ~6 分，是采样边界\n")
        f.write("# step_back: busy分钟 x 在跑均值 / 产出token（导出量；用 busy 不用墙钟，见下）; step_perreq: 逐请求 decode 间隔中位（独立测量）\n")
        w = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=HEADER)
        w.writeheader()
        w.writerows(rows)
    print("写入 %s：%d 行" % (out_path, len(rows)))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""round-1+ 命中率序列 —— paper/data/hitrate.csv。

为什么要有：§related 引用「无 HiCache 时 80.8/78.5/70.6/56.0/32.8%，有 HiCache 时
80.7/79.6/78.7%，控制器 80.6%」以及「计划值 80.9%」。这一串数是从 steps.jsonl 的
server_prefix_hit_rate 逐步算的，此前不在任何 CSV 里（审计报了 8 处"找不到源头"）。

⚠ 口径（正文必须与表头一致，混了会被审稿人抓）：
  这是【观测】的 server 命中率，round>=1 的【逐步非加权均值】。
  它与 gen_reuse.py 的 reuse_frac 是同一现象的不同归一化：
    - 本表：只取 round>=1，逐步非加权
    - reuse_frac：全部步，按 prompt token 加权
  两者不可互相换算，也不能混引。计划的 round-1+ 值恒为 80.9%，不随 cap 变。

用法: python3 gen_hitrate.py <results_dir> <out.csv> <run> [<run> ...]
"""
import csv, json, os, statistics, sys


def round1_mean(path):
    st = [json.loads(l) for l in open(path) if l.strip()]
    r1 = [s for s in st if s.get("round_idx", 0) >= 1]
    if not r1:
        return None, None, 0
    obs = statistics.mean(s.get("server_prefix_hit_rate") or 0 for s in r1)
    plan = statistics.mean(s.get("planned_prefix_hit_rate") or 0 for s in r1)
    return obs, plan, len(r1)


def main():
    root, out_p = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2]
    runs = sys.argv[3:]
    rows = []
    for r in runs:
        p = os.path.join(root, r, "steps.jsonl")
        if not os.path.exists(p):
            print("!! 缺 %s" % p); continue
        obs, plan, n = round1_mean(p)
        if obs is None:
            print("!! %s 没有 round>=1 的步" % r); continue
        rows.append({"run": r, "n_round1_steps": n,
                     "observed_round1_pct": "%.1f" % (obs * 100),
                     "planned_round1_pct": "%.2f" % (plan * 100)})
    header = "\n".join([
        "# round-1+ 命中率 —— 由 scripts/lab/gen_hitrate.py 生成，勿手改",
        "# 口径：观测的 server_prefix_hit_rate，round>=1，逐步【非加权均值】",
        "# ⚠ 与 reuse.csv 的 reuse_frac 不是一回事（那个是全部步、按 token 加权），",
        "#   两者不可换算、不可混引。planned_round1_pct 是 trace 常量，不随 cap 变。",
        "run,n_round1_steps,observed_round1_pct,planned_round1_pct"])
    fields = ["run", "n_round1_steps", "observed_round1_pct", "planned_round1_pct"]
    body = header + "\n" + "\n".join(",".join(str(x[k]) for k in fields) for x in rows) + "\n"
    assert isinstance(body, str)
    with open(out_p, "w") as fh:
        fh.write(body)
    print("写入 %s：%d 行" % (out_p, len(rows)))
    for x in rows:
        print("  %-20s round>=1 n=%3d  观测 %5s%%  计划 %s%%" % (
            x["run"], x["n_round1_steps"], x["observed_round1_pct"], x["planned_round1_pct"]))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""轮次间隔：trace 要求的 vs 实测的 —— paper/data/toolwait.csv。

为什么要有：论文摘要里那句「the wait averages 16.85 s, not the sub-second figure
the trace's median suggests」的数字【一度不在任何 CSV 里】（2026-09-24 审计发现）。
按本项目纪律，摘要级的数字必须有出处。

口径（必须写清，否则差一个分母就得到完全不同的均值 —— 我自己就先算错过一次）：
  轮次转换 = 【同会话相邻两轮】。等价于「每个会话除最后一轮之外的每一行」，
  因为某一轮的 tool_wait_after_ms 就是到下一轮的等待。**每个会话的最后一轮
  没有后继，必须排除**：不排除的话分母是 999（全部行）而均值只有 12.65 s，
  排除后分母是 799 而均值 15.82 s。这两个数差 25%，全在于分母。
  （trace 的 tool_wait_after_ms 又只有 662 个正值 —— 把零值也滤掉均值变成 19.10 s，
   那是第三种口径。三种都不是错的，但正文必须指明用的是哪一种。）

  要求值 = trace 的 tool_wait_after_ms（重放管线要等这么久）
  实测值 = run 的 steps.jsonl 里，上一轮 complete_timestamp 到下一轮
           submit_timestamp 的实际间隔（含等 permit 的门时间）

用法: python3 gen_toolwait.py <trace.csv> <results_dir> <out.csv> <run> [<run> ...]
"""
import csv, collections, datetime, json, os, statistics, sys


def pct(v, p):
    s = sorted(v)
    return s[min(len(s) - 1, int(p * len(s)))]


def transitions(trace):
    """返回 [(session_id, round_idx, tool_wait_after_ms)]，只含同会话相邻轮。"""
    rows = list(csv.DictReader(open(trace)))
    mx = collections.defaultdict(int)
    for r in rows:
        mx[r["session_id"]] = max(mx[r["session_id"]], int(r["round_idx"]))
    out = [(r["session_id"], int(r["round_idx"]), float(r["tool_wait_after_ms"]))
           for r in rows if int(r["round_idx"]) < mx[r["session_id"]]]
    assert len(out) == len(rows) - len(mx), \
        "轮次转换数 %d != 行数 %d - 会话数 %d" % (len(out), len(rows), len(mx))
    return out, len(rows), len(mx)


def measured(steps_path):
    """实测间隔：同一会话里，上一轮 complete 到下一轮 submit。"""
    st = [json.loads(l) for l in open(steps_path) if l.strip()]
    by = collections.defaultdict(dict)
    for s in st:
        by[s["session_id"]][s["round_idx"]] = s
    out = []
    for sid, d in by.items():
        for k in sorted(d):
            if k + 1 in d:
                out.append((d[k + 1]["submit_timestamp"] - d[k]["complete_timestamp"]) * 1000.0)
    return out


def main():
    trace, root, out_p = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], sys.argv[3]
    runs = sys.argv[4:]
    tr, nrows, nsess = transitions(trace)
    dem = [w for _, _, w in tr]

    rows = []
    for r in runs:
        sp = os.path.join(root, r, "steps.jsonl")
        if not os.path.exists(sp):
            print("!! 缺 %s" % sp)
            continue
        me = measured(sp)
        assert len(me) == len(dem), \
            "%s: 实测转换数 %d != trace 的 %d —— 分母不一致，均值不可比" % (r, len(me), len(dem))
        rows.append({"run": r, "n_transitions": len(me),
                     "demand_med_s": "%.2f" % (statistics.median(dem) / 1000),
                     "demand_mean_s": "%.2f" % (statistics.mean(dem) / 1000),
                     "demand_p90_s": "%.2f" % (pct(dem, 0.9) / 1000),
                     "measured_med_s": "%.2f" % (statistics.median(me) / 1000),
                     "measured_mean_s": "%.2f" % (statistics.mean(me) / 1000),
                     "measured_p90_s": "%.2f" % (pct(me, 0.9) / 1000)})

    header = "\n".join([
        "# trace: %s（%d 行 - %d 会话 = %d 次转换）" % (os.path.basename(trace), nrows, nsess, len(dem)),
        "# 轮次间隔：trace 要求 vs 实测 —— 由 scripts/lab/gen_toolwait.py 生成，勿手改",
        "# 轮次转换 = 同会话相邻两轮 = 每会话除最后一轮外的每一行",
        "# ⚠ 分母不同，均值差很多，正文引用时必须指明口径：",
        "#     全部 999 行（含每会话最后一轮，无后继）  均值 12.65 s",
        "#     799 次轮次转换（本表）                  均值 15.82 s   <- 论文用的",
        "#     662 个非零 tool_wait                     均值 19.10 s",
        "# 论文摘要的 16.85 s 是【实测】列的均值（run 的 steps.jsonl 实测间隔，",
        "# 含等 permit 的门时间），15.82 s 是 trace 要求值。",
        "run,n_transitions,demand_med_s,demand_mean_s,demand_p90_s,measured_med_s,measured_mean_s,measured_p90_s"])
    fields = ["run", "n_transitions", "demand_med_s", "demand_mean_s", "demand_p90_s",
              "measured_med_s", "measured_mean_s", "measured_p90_s"]
    body = header + "\n" + "\n".join(",".join(str(r[k]) for k in fields) for r in rows) + "\n"
    assert isinstance(body, str)
    with open(out_p, "w") as fh:
        fh.write(body)

    print("轮次转换 %d（%d 行 - %d 会话）" % (len(dem), nrows, nsess))
    print("要求值: 中位 %.2f s  均值 %.2f s  p90 %.2f s" % (
        statistics.median(dem) / 1000, statistics.mean(dem) / 1000, pct(dem, 0.9) / 1000))
    for r in rows:
        print("  %-24s 实测 中位 %5s  均值 %5s  p90 %5s" % (
            r["run"], r["measured_med_s"], r["measured_mean_s"], r["measured_p90_s"]))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

    15	configuration where \S\ref{sec:model} says added concurrency is expensive. The two workloads differ in ways that matter for
    16	this paper, which is why we chose it. Its sessions are smaller (median prompt
    17	18{,}809 tokens against 23{,}187) and its tool waits are far longer at the
    18	median: over round transitions---same session,
    19	adjacent rounds, each session's last round excluded because it has no
    20	successor---the gap is 20.2\,s against 0.34\,s at the median and 39.9\,s
    21	against 15.8\,s at the mean, roughly 60$\times$ either way
    22	(\texttt{toolwait.csv} carries both traces on this definition). The 29.1\,s
    23	that appears in \texttt{elasticity\_x.csv} is the mean over all 390 rows
    24	including each session's last round, and is not comparable with the main
    25	trace's 15.8\,s; we use the transition definition throughout §\ref{sec:xtrace}. So the engine is idle far more of the
    26	time, and the running counts are lower.
    27	
    28	\subsection{What transfers, and what does not}
    29	
    30	\paragraph{The reuse accounting transfers in direction, not in level.} The
    31	second trace plans a higher reuse bound than the first---86.3\% of its prompt
    32	tokens against 75.5\%---and realizes less of it, at a lower device occupancy.
    33	Realization falls from 74.8\% to 64.0\% as the running count rises from 1.08 to
    34	1.41, so the direction of \S\ref{sec:model} survives. What does \emph{not}
    35	survive is the shape. On the main trace the decline is a threshold: the
    36	per-unit slope is $-10.8$, $-41.0$, then $-157.4$ and $-126.7$ points per unit
    37	as the running count passes 1.53, which is the cliff \S\ref{sec:criterion}
    38	measures. Here the slope is roughly constant---$-31.3$, $-36.6$, $-28.4$---over

     1	\section{Decomposing the Wall Clock}
     2	\label{sec:model}
     3	
     4	\subsection{Two components}
     5	
     6	We split the wall clock at every sampled instant according to whether the
     7	engine has any request in flight:
     8	\begin{equation}
     9	  W \;=\; W_{\text{busy}} \;+\; W_{\text{idle}} .
    10	  \label{eq:split}
    11	\end{equation}
    12	Fig.~\ref{fig:decomp} shows the split for every completed run. Two things are
    13	immediately visible and both matter later.
    14	
    15	First, idle time is not noise. It ranges from 7.4\% of the fastest
    16	configuration's sampled span to 36.3\% of the slowest's---the denominator here
    17	is the span the sampler observed, which runs about six minutes longer than the
    18	request wall clock of Table~\ref{tab:runs}, so the fractions against that
    19	wall clock are 7.6\% and 36.7\%---and it is not spread evenly.
    20	Over 1{,}849 stretches the mean is 20.6\,s and 17.6\% of them exceed
    21	20\,s, accounting for 69.6\% of all idle \emph{time}
    22	(Fig.~\ref{fig:idle}). The tail, not the middle, is what a scheduler feels,
    23	and it sits at the scale of the 15.8--16.9\,s mean tool wait (demanded and

    25	than the median here because the median is not robust to which runs enter the

    27	that one run contributes 411 of the 1{,}849 stretches and is itself the

    29	(64.8--70.6\%) move by little across the four poolings we report in

    32	(25.1--30.1\,s) and we give it as a range for the same reason.
    33	
    34	Second, the two components move independently. \texttt{bres}
    35	(\S\ref{sec:results}) reduces idle from 15.9\% to 10.1\%, recovering about 17

# running_all: metrics.csv 的 sglang:num_running_reqs 全时段均值
# running_busy: running_all / busy占比 —— 论文 §III 与 §IV 一律用这个

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""九条控制器臂的 **busy 条件** 在跑数均值 —— 论文 §IV 与归因表必须用同一个口径。

为什么需要：§IV 原来写「九条臂跑在 1.35~1.69」，而那个列表是**硬编码**在
gen_elasticity.py 里、注释还指错了文件（说「见 occupancy.csv」，但
occupancy.csv 的 running_mean 是全时段均值，范围 0.97~1.41，根本不含 1.63+）。
同一篇稿子两页之后的归因表又用了全时段均值当 R̄ 列。同一个符号两个口径。

busy 条件均值 = 全时段均值 / busy 占比，与 gen_decomp.py 里 τ₁ 的分母一致。

用法: python3 gen_arm_running.py <results_dir> <out.csv>
"""
import csv, os, statistics, sys

# sf0_r2 与 hc_r2 必须在里面：归因表引用 sf0 的在跑数，而它原来不在本表里，
# gen_attribution 于是静默回退到 occupancy.csv 的【全时段均值】口径（1.26），
# 而其余 11 行都是 busy 条件口径（sf0 的 busy 值是 1.45）—— 口径混用（2026-09-24 审稿人抓）。
# 这就是 CLAUDE.md 纪律 3 的又一个实例：生成器的 run 列表不全会静默改变下游口径。
ARMS = ["foyer_sf0_r2", "foyer_hc", "foyer_hc_r2", "foyer_hz1", "foyer_hz2",
        "foyer_hz0", "foyer_bres", "foyer_breshz1", "foyer_nogate", "foyer_rg4",
        "foyfix_hc", "aimd2", "aimd2_paper"]
STATIC = ["cap1", "cap2", "cap3_fixed", "cap4", "cap5",
          "pairB_cap2hc", "cap3_hc", "cap4_hc", "cap4_hc_r2"]


def one(path):
    rd = list(csv.DictReader(open(path)))
    rn = [float(x["sglang:num_running_reqs"]) for x in rd if x["sglang:num_running_reqs"]]
    if not rn:
        return None
    allm = statistics.mean(rn)
    busy_frac = sum(1 for v in rn if v > 0) / len(rn)
    return allm, (allm / busy_frac if busy_frac else 0.0), busy_frac


def main():
    root, out = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2]
    rows = []
    print("%-16s %8s %9s %8s" % ("run", "R̄全时", "R̄busy", "busy占比"))
    for group, names in (("controller", ARMS), ("static", STATIC)):
        for r in names:
            p = os.path.join(root, "pois200_" + r, "metrics.csv")
            if not os.path.exists(p):
                continue
            res = one(p)
            if not res:
                continue
            a, b, f = res
            rows.append({"run": r, "family": group, "running_all": round(a, 3),
                         "running_busy": round(b, 3), "busy_frac": round(f, 3)})
            print("%-16s %8.2f %9.2f %8.3f" % (r, a, b, f))
    with open(out, "w", newline="") as f:
        f.write("# 在跑数的两个口径 —— 由 scripts/lab/gen_arm_running.py 生成\n")
        f.write("# running_all: metrics.csv 的 sglang:num_running_reqs 全时段均值\n")
        f.write("# running_busy: running_all / busy占比 —— 论文 §III 与 §IV 一律用这个\n")
        f.write("# 论文里引用 R̄ 时必须指明是哪一个；两个口径差 1.3~1.7 倍\n")
        w = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=list(rows[0].keys()))
        w.writeheader(); w.writerows(rows)
    ctrl = [r["running_busy"] for r in rows if r["family"] == "controller"]
    if ctrl:
        print()
        print("控制器臂 R̄busy: %.2f ~ %.2f（%d 条）" % (min(ctrl), max(ctrl), len(ctrl)))
        print("  >=1.53 的: %d 条" % sum(1 for x in ctrl if x >= 1.53))
    print("->", out)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""把 run 放到静态前沿上判「赢了没有」。

用法:  python3 frontier.py <run> [<run> ...]
       python3 frontier.py --all-foyer

为什么需要它：我们反复在"这个数比那个数好"上打转，而正确的判据只有一个——
**这个点在不在这条 (墙钟, SLO) 静态前沿的上方**。落在两个静态 cap 中间的连线上
不算赢，因为静态方法按时间片混合就能逼近线上任一点（§二十七、§五十二）。

前沿的三个锚点（全部 999 步、HiCache 开、同一代码代）：
    cap4_hc_r2 (209.1, 68.5)   # 重复 run 支配了 cap4_hc，见 paper/data/runs.csv
    cap3_hc_r2 (230.3, 81.9)   # 2026-09-24 更正：cap3_hc 的重复同样在两个轴上都支配它，
                               # 按同一条规矩必须顶掉。原稿以"它超了自己的 cap"为由排除，
                               # 但那是【另一条非 HiCache 的 pois200_cap3_r2】（峰值在飞 5、
                               # 超帽 126 个样本）；cap3_hc_r2 峰值在飞 3、超帽 0，是干净的。
    cap2_hc  (295.0, 91.6)   <- pairB；pairA (304.5, 90.6) 被它支配，不入前沿
低于 213 分钟没有任何静态点，落在那里是平凡获胜。
"""
import json, os, statistics as st, sys

RESULTS = "/data/xbw/turnstile/results/night"
# 上左包络的锚点，按墙钟升序。cap1 没有 HiCache 版本，但它仍然是"静态 cap 能达到的点"，
# 必须放进前沿——否则墙钟超过最右锚点的 run 会被误判成"越过前沿"（cap1 自己就撞过这个）。
FRONT = [(209.1, 68.5, "cap4_hc_r2"), (230.3, 81.9, "cap3_hc_r2"),
         (295.0, 91.6, "cap2_hc"), (527.4, 94.8, "cap1")]


def front_slo(wall):
    """前沿在给定墙钟处的 SLO 上界（分段线性插值）。超出锚点范围返回 None——
    范围外没有可比的前沿，不能给判决，否则端点外推会凭空造出"越过"。"""
    if wall < FRONT[0][0] or wall > FRONT[-1][0]:
        return None
    for (w0, s0, _), (w1, s1, _) in zip(FRONT, FRONT[1:]):
        if w0 <= wall <= w1:
            return s0 + (s1 - s0) * (wall - w0) / (w1 - w0)
    return None


def summarise(run):
    p = os.path.join(RESULTS, run, "steps.jsonl")
    if not os.path.exists(p):
        return None
    rows = [json.loads(l) for l in open(p)]
    ts = [r["submit_timestamp"] for r in rows if r.get("submit_timestamp")]
    te = [r["complete_timestamp"] for r in rows if r.get("complete_timestamp")]
    tt = [r["first_token_ms"] for r in rows if r.get("first_token_ms") is not None]
    if not ts or not tt:
        return None
    wall = (max(te) - min(ts)) / 60.0
    slo = 100.0 * sum(1 for x in tt if x <= 10000) / len(tt)
    # 实测并发：按 30 秒采样活跃请求数
    N = 300
    act = []
    for i in range(N):
        t = min(ts) + (max(te) - min(ts)) * i / (N - 1)
        act.append(sum(1 for r in rows if r.get("submit_timestamp") and r.get("complete_timestamp")
                       and r["submit_timestamp"] <= t < r["complete_timestamp"]))
    # 分段 SLO 标准差（十等分）
    seg = []
    for i in range(10):
        s = tt[i * len(tt) // 10:(i + 1) * len(tt) // 10]
        if s:
            seg.append(100.0 * sum(1 for x in s if x <= 10000) / len(s))
    sd = st.pstdev(seg) if len(seg) > 1 else 0.0
    return dict(run=run, steps=len(rows), wall=wall, conc=sum(act) / N, slo=slo, sd=sd)


def verdict(d):
    f = front_slo(d["wall"])
    d["front"] = float("nan") if f is None else f
    if f is None:
        d["delta"] = float("nan")
        d["verdict"] = ("LEFT-OF-FRONT (无静态点在更左, 平凡获胜)"
                        if d["wall"] < FRONT[0][0] else "OUT-OF-RANGE (超出现有静态点范围)")
        return d
    d["delta"] = d["slo"] - f
    if d["delta"] > 1.0:
        d["verdict"] = "ABOVE  (越过前沿)"
    elif d["delta"] > -1.0:
        d["verdict"] = "ON     (在线上, 不算赢)"
    else:
        d["verdict"] = "BELOW  (在线下)"
    return d


def emit_csv():
    """全精度导出 —— 给 gen_table.py 用。

    为什么需要：runs.csv 是手抄 run_summary.py 打印的【1 位小数】表得来的，
    而本脚本从原始日志算的是全精度。同一条插值公式，输入 263.3/86.3 与
    263.340318/86.286286 会给出差 0.02pp 的 delta —— 于是表 I 写 -0.55、
    正文写 -0.57（2026-09-24 审稿人抓到的对不上）。让两条管线共用这一个
    全精度来源，而不是各自去读不同精度的表。
    """
    import csv as _csv
    w = _csv.writer(sys.stdout)
    w.writerow(["run", "wall_min", "slo_pct"])
    for nm in sorted(os.listdir(RESULTS)):
        if not nm.startswith("pois200_"):
            continue
        d = summarise(nm)
        if not d or d["steps"] < 999:
            continue
        w.writerow([nm, "%.6f" % d["wall"], "%.6f" % d["slo"]])


def main():
    runs = sys.argv[1:]
    if runs == ["--csv"]:
        emit_csv()
        return 0
    if runs == ["--all-foyer"]:
        runs = sorted(r for r in os.listdir(RESULTS)
                      if r.startswith("pois200_foyer") or r.startswith("pois200_cap"))
    out = []
    for r in runs:
        d = summarise(r)
        if d and d["steps"] >= 999:
            out.append(verdict(d))
    print("%-30s %5s %8s %7s %7s %8s %8s  %s" %
          ("run", "步数", "墙钟min", "并发", "SLO%", "前沿SLO", "差(pp)", "判决"))
    for d in sorted(out, key=lambda x: x["wall"]):
        print("%-30s %5d %8.1f %7.2f %7.1f %8.1f %+8.2f  %s" %
              (d["run"], d["steps"], d["wall"], d["conc"], d["slo"], d["front"], d["delta"], d["verdict"]))
    print("\n注意：步数 < 999 的 run 一律不列（未跑完的不能与跑完的比，§多处的教训）。")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
     8	open.
     9	
    10	We run the same controller and the same cap family on a 25-session tier. The
    11	throttle configuration is reversed: with all safety throttles disabled, the
    12	25-session tier runs \emph{51\% faster} (58.4 to 28.8 minutes) at a cost of
    13	only 2.4\,pp of SLO, while on the 200-session tier disabling the same
    14	throttles costs 42\% (291.3 to 415.0 minutes) and 43.7\,pp of SLO. One switch,
    15	two regimes, opposite signs, both around 40--50\%
    16	(Fig.~\ref{fig:loadtier}). No single scalar setting is optimal across both.
    17	
    18	We flag the generation gap rather than let the figure imply more than we ran.
    52	\end{table}
    53	
    54	The finding is a trade, not a defeat, and it is the opposite of what the
    55	200-session tier shows. Against the same-generation envelope---which runs from
    56	\texttt{cap4} at (23.6, 79.2) through \texttt{cap3} at (24.2, 91.2) to
    57	\texttt{cap2} at (32.4, 92.8)---\sys{} sits $+0.9$\,pp above the line. A
    58	static cap of three is still 12\% faster in wall clock, but it gives up
    59	1.6\,pp of SLO to do it, so neither dominates the other. On this tier the
    60	controller is a different point on the front rather than a dominated one,
    61	which is exactly what the 200-session tier denies it.
    62	
    63	We are careful about the size, and about the yardstick. $+0.9$\,pp is
    64	measured against a straight-line interpolation between static points, not
    65	against a switching policy anyone ran, so the defensible statement is that
    66	\sys{} is not dominated by any static point we measured on this tier---not
    67	that it beats what a static mix could achieve. On 125 requests one request is
    68	worth 0.8\,pp, so the margin is about one request. The tier's
    69	segment-to-segment SD (5.1 to 21.4\,pp) is not the right yardstick: segment
    70	volatility is not run-to-run uncertainty. The right one is the replicate spread
    71	we have---which, across the four configurations we repeated, is 0.5, 1.0, 3.9
    72	and 5.1\,pp. Against the two tightest of those $+0.9$\,pp is about a replicate
    73	wide; against the two widest---which belong to the configurations that decide
    74	our headline elsewhere---it is well inside one. We quote the tightest pair
    75	because it is the least favourable to us, not because it is the whole
    76	distribution. What we will defend is the weaker claim: on the
    77	low-load tier the controller is not worse, and the same switch that is worth
    78	$+51\%$ there costs $-42\%$ at high load. A controller that is competitive
    79	where load is light and dominated where load is heavy is the pattern this
    80	paper set out to explain, and it is the pattern we measured.
   258	loss is systematic: tight caps queue the hardest, so \texttt{cap1} falls from
   259	94.8\% to 76.2\% while \texttt{cap5} falls only from 27.1\% to 24.2\%. A
   260	session admitted last under \texttt{cap1} can wait hours for its turn; its
   261	engine-side TTFT is still 1.6\,s.
   262	
   263	What does \emph{not} change is the verdict, within the subset we recomputed.
   264	The static envelope keeps the same four anchors, and of the eleven controller
   265	arms, nine move by no more than
   266	2.7\,pp and none crosses the $\pm1.0$\,pp band into a new verdict. Two move
   267	more: the all-throttles-off arm improves by 8.3\,pp and the retuned AIMD arm by
   268	2.8\,pp, both of which stay below the front either way. The one that decides our headline moves the wrong way for
   269	the controller: on the ready clock \texttt{horizon}=1's own SLO falls from
   270	84.8\% to 68.2\% while the anchor it is measured against falls less, so the
   271	ready clock moves it further below the line rather than closer to it. We do not
   272	give a ready-clock distance for it, because the two anchor repeats that define
   273	the line are not in the set we could recompute (\S\ref{sec:discussion} says
   274	which runs are), and inventing a line for them would be the same error this
   195	The remaining 54.8\% are cycles where the controller selected no candidate at
   196	all, and here we are explicit about the limit of what we can resolve. The
   197	controller is single-flight by default: it authorizes at most one session per
   198	two-second cycle and waits for that session to begin a round before authorizing
   199	the next. Whether that gate held or whether a candidate was tried and did not
   200	fit is \emph{not distinguishable from our logs}---the controller does not record
   201	it---so we do not attribute the bucket to either. We bounded it by running that arm: the same configuration with single-flight
   202	disabled. Two things came out of it, and the second is the one that matters.
   203	
   204	The bound first. With the gate off, the residual bucket---which can then only
   205	be \emph{candidates that did not fit}, since the gate cannot be holding---falls
   206	from 54.8\% of blocked cycles to 39.7\%, against 44.0\% that are over budget
   207	outright. So the single-flight path accounts for roughly fifteen points, and
   208	the headroom estimate remains the largest single cause. (Both arms' numbers
   209	are rows of \texttt{gate\_attrib.csv}, which now includes the \texttt{sf=0}
   210	arm; an earlier revision quoted 39.9 and 43.5 for the second pair, values that
   211	appeared in no data file.)
   212	
   213	The second thing is that disabling it does not make the controller win.

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""空转段的合并统计（全部 vs 排除受争用影响的 run）—— paper/data/idle_pooled.csv。

为什么要有：论文 §III 引用「1849 段，中位 5.6 s，均值 20.6 s，idle 时间中 69.6%
在 >20 s 的段里」。但那 1849 段里含四条被同机争用影响的 run（争用抬高 τ，
busy 变长、idle 变短，所以段时长【可能】被影响）。本脚本回答：排除它们之后
这些数会变吗？

答案是不会（中位 5.60→5.00 s，均值 20.65→21.02 s，>20 s 占比 69.6%→70.6%）。
这是因为段时长由【工具等待】决定，是工作负载属性，不是步成本的函数 ——
所以这个检查反过来支持了论文「空转是结构性的」这一主张。

用法: python3 gen_idle_pooled.py paper/data/idle_seg_raw.csv paper/data/concurrency.csv <out.csv>
"""
import csv, statistics, sys

BAD = None


def load(p):
    return [r for r in csv.DictReader(l for l in open(p) if not l.startswith("#"))]


def main():
    raw_p, conc_p, out_p = sys.argv[1:4]
    bad = {r["run"].replace("pois200_", "") for r in load(conc_p)
           if r["verdict"] == "CONTESTED"}
    rows = load(raw_p)
    assert len(rows) > 1000, "原始段只有 %d 条，路径或格式不对" % len(rows)

    # 四个变体都出：合并在位/均值对【并哪些 run】的敏感性本身就是要报的东西。
    # 实测：中位被 cap1 一条主导（它占 22% 的段，自身中位 5.0 s），去掉它中位
    # 从 5.60 跳到 10.00；而均值（18.3~21.0）与 >20s 占比（64.8~70.6%）稳定。
    damaged = {"foyer_hz2", "foyer_hz0", "foyer_bres"}   # 实测被争用损害的三条
    out = []
    for name, excl in (("all_runs", set()),
                       ("minus_cap1", {"cap1"}),
                       ("minus_damaged3", damaged),
                       ("minus_contested4", bad & {r["run"] for r in rows})):
        sel = [r for r in rows if r["run"] not in excl]
        v = [float(r["duration_s"]) for r in sel]
        tot = sum(v)
        out.append({
            "set": name, "n_segments": len(v), "n_runs": len({r["run"] for r in sel}),
            "median_s": "%.2f" % statistics.median(v),
            "mean_s": "%.2f" % statistics.mean(v),
            "p90_s": "%.2f" % sorted(v)[int(0.9 * len(v))],
            "max_s": "%.1f" % max(v),
            "pct_idle_time_in_gt20s": "%.1f" % (100 * sum(x for x in v if x > 20) / tot),
            "pct_segments_gt20s": "%.1f" % (100 * sum(1 for x in v if x > 20) / len(v)),
        })

    header = "\n".join([
        "# 空转段的合并统计 —— 由 scripts/lab/gen_idle_pooled.py 生成，勿手改",
        "# clean_only = 排除 concurrency.csv 里判 CONTESTED 的 run（它们的 τ 被同机争用抬高）",
        "# 四个变体：中位与 p90 被 cap1 主导（它占 22% 的段），均值与 >20s 占比稳定。",
        "# 论文引用的「中位 5.6 s」必须写成区间；「均值 ~20 s」与「>20s 占 idle 时间 ~2/3」稳健。",
        "set,n_segments,n_runs,median_s,mean_s,p90_s,max_s,pct_idle_time_in_gt20s,pct_segments_gt20s"])
    fields = ["set", "n_segments", "n_runs", "median_s", "mean_s", "p90_s", "max_s",
              "pct_idle_time_in_gt20s", "pct_segments_gt20s"]
    body = header + "\n" + "\n".join(",".join(str(r[k]) for k in fields) for r in out) + "\n"
    assert isinstance(body, str)
    with open(out_p, "w") as fh:
        fh.write(body)
    print("写入 %s" % out_p)
    for r in out:
        print("  %-11s n=%4d(%2d run) 中位 %5s 均值 %5s  p90 %6s  >20s 占 idle 时间 %5s%%"
              % (r["set"], r["n_segments"], r["n_runs"], r["median_s"], r["mean_s"],
                 r["p90_s"], r["pct_idle_time_in_gt20s"]))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""生成 paper/data/eviction.csv —— 论文 §3.3 里「驱逐 → 重算」那条因果链的数据。

为什么要有这个脚本：这条链子曾经被错误地"反驳"过，用的两个检验都是恒等式：

  1. "total prefilled tokens == planned prefill, ratio 1.00"
     `sglang:prompt_tokens_total` 计的是**完整提交的 prompt 长度，含 cached 部分**，
     所以它恒等于 sum(prompt_len)，不管重算多少都不会变。这是个恒等式。
     能测出重算的是 `server_uncached_prompt_tokens`，本脚本报的就是它。

  2. "SGLang reports zero retractions"
     radix cache 驱逐记在 `sglang:evicted_tokens_total`；retraction 指的是
     抢占**正在跑**的请求，是另一件事、另一个计数器。拿后者反驳前者是范畴错误。

正确的读法（本脚本的输出）：
  - 设备侧未命中 = 设备没接住的量（prompt − cached_tokens_total）
  - 重算量 = steps 里的 server_uncached_prompt_tokens（真正重新 prefill 的）
  - 两者之差 = host 梯队接住的量
  - 无 HiCache 的五条：host 接住【精确为 0】，设备未命中 == 重算量，1:1
  - 有 HiCache 的八条：host 接住 13~19M
  - 但 HiCache 那一族的 τ 仍然散开（重算恒定 8.7M，τ 22.5~30.6ms）→ 未解释

用法: python3 gen_eviction.py <results_dir> <out.csv> [run ...]
"""
import csv, json, os, statistics, sys, glob

HEADER = ["run", "hicache", "prompt_M", "dev_miss_M", "host_hit_M", "uncached_M",
          "evicted_M", "running", "step_back_ms", "step_perreq_ms",
          # 下面三列是【未舍入】的原始计数，专供下游算比例用。
          # 为什么必须单列：曾经让下游拿舍入到 0.1M 的列去算百分比，cap1 于是印成
          # 69.8% 而真值是 19,934,847/28,526,871 = 69.9%（2026-09-24 审稿人从原始
          # 日志独立求和抓到的）。凡是要算比例的，一律走这三列。
          "prompt_tok", "reuse_tok", "uncached_tok",
          "dev_miss_tok", "host_hit_tok"]
SAMPLE_S = 5.0

RUNS = ["cap1", "cap2", "cap3_fixed", "cap4", "cap5", "pairB_cap2hc", "cap3_hc",
        "cap4_hc", "foyer_hc", "foyer_hz1", "foyer_hz2", "foyer_hz0", "foyer_bres"]


def last_value(path, name):
    """取某一列最后一个有效值（这些是累计计数器）。"""
    hdr = open(path).readline().strip().split(",")
    if name not in hdr:
        return None
    i = hdr.index(name)
    v = None
    for line in open(path).readlines()[1:]:
        c = line.rstrip("\n").split(",")
        if len(c) <= i:
            continue
        try:
            v = float(c[i])
        except ValueError:
            pass
    return v


def col(path, name):
    hdr = open(path).readline().strip().split(",")
    if name not in hdr:
        return []
    i = hdr.index(name)
    out = []
    for line in open(path).readlines()[1:]:
        c = line.rstrip("\n").split(",")
        if len(c) <= i:
            continue
        try:
            out.append(float(c[i]))
        except ValueError:
            pass
    return out


def main():
    root, out_path = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2]
    runs = sys.argv[3:] or RUNS
    rows, bad, missing = [], [], []
    for r in runs:
        d = os.path.join(root, "pois200_" + r)
        mp = os.path.join(d, "metrics.csv")
        sp = os.path.join(d, "steps.jsonl")
        if not (os.path.exists(mp) and os.path.exists(sp)):
            missing.append(r)
            continue
        try:
            prompt = last_value(mp, "sglang:prompt_tokens_total")
            cached = last_value(mp, "sglang:cached_tokens_total")
            evicted = last_value(mp, "sglang:evicted_tokens_total")
            steps = [json.loads(l) for l in open(sp) if l.strip()]
            uncached = sum(s.get("server_uncached_prompt_tokens") or 0 for s in steps)
            plan = sum(s.get("prompt_len") or 0 for s in steps)

            # 断言 1：prompt_tokens_total 必须等于计划 prefill —— 这是恒等式，
            # 挡住的是列读错（读错列不会有这个性质）
            assert prompt and abs(prompt - plan) / plan < 0.01, \
                "prompt_tokens_total %.1fM 与计划 %.1fM 不符，列可能读错了" % (
                    (prompt or 0) / 1e6, plan / 1e6)

            # 断言 2：cached 必须是 prompt 的一部分（不是独立的量）
            assert cached is not None and 0 <= cached <= prompt * 1.01, \
                "cached_tokens_total 不在 [0, prompt] 内"
            # 注意：prompt − cached == steps 的未命中数，这条【只在无 HiCache 时成立】。
            # 有 HiCache 时两者差很多（cap4_hc: 22.4M vs 9.1M），因为
            # sglang:cached_tokens_total 只计【设备侧】radix cache 的命中，
            # host 梯队接住的那部分不进这个计数器，但逐请求 usage 里算作 cached。
            # 所以本表以 steps 的未命中数为准（那才是真正重新 prefill 的量），
            # 并把设备侧命中单列出来，两者的差就是 host 梯队的贡献。
            dev_miss = prompt - cached   # 设备侧未命中

            rn = col(mp, "sglang:num_running_reqs")
            # HiCache 判定：优先读 metadata 的 build.hicache_enabled（新 run 都有），
            # 读不到再退回硬编码集合。**不能只靠硬编码** —— 原来那份漏了
            # foyer_breshz1（它 host 接住 14.4M，确实是 host-tier run），
            # 于是 §III 的"八条 host-tier run"其实是九条，报的区间是从少一个
            # 成员的集合算的，而漏掉的那个恰是最极端的一点（2026-09-24 审稿人抓）。
            HC_FALLBACK = {"pairB_cap2hc", "cap3_hc", "cap4_hc", "foyer_hc",
                           "foyer_hz1", "foyer_hz2", "foyer_hz0", "foyer_bres",
                           "foyer_breshz1"}
            _mj = os.path.join(d, "metadata.json")
            hc_flag = None
            if os.path.exists(_mj):
                try:
                    hc_flag = json.load(open(_mj)).get("build", {}).get("hicache_enabled")
                except Exception:
                    hc_flag = None
            if hc_flag is None:
                hicache = r in HC_FALLBACK
            else:
                hicache = bool(hc_flag)
            rows.append({
                "run": r,
                "hicache": "1" if hicache else "0",
                "prompt_M": round(prompt / 1e6, 1),

                "uncached_M": round(uncached / 1e6, 1),
                "evicted_M": round((evicted or 0) / 1e6, 1),
                "dev_miss_M": round(dev_miss / 1e6, 1),
                "host_hit_M": round((dev_miss - uncached) / 1e6, 1),   # 设备没接住、但也没重算的
                "running": round(statistics.mean(rn), 2) if rn else "",
                "step_back_ms": "", "step_perreq_ms": "",
                "prompt_tok": int(prompt),
                "reuse_tok": int(prompt - uncached),
                "uncached_tok": int(uncached),
                "dev_miss_tok": int(dev_miss),
                "host_hit_tok": int(dev_miss - uncached),
            })
        except Exception as e:
            bad.append((r, str(e)))

    if missing:
        print("!! 以下 run 在 %s 下不存在（镜像不全？）—— 静默丢掉会让数据文件的集合" % root)
        print("!! 与正文引用的集合不一致（2026-09-24 就这样漏掉过 foyer_hz1）：")
        for r in missing:
            print("   %s" % r)
    if bad:
        print("!! 未通过校验：")
        for r, why in bad:
            print("   %-14s %s" % (r, why))

    with open(out_path, "w", newline="") as f:
        f.write("# 驱逐 vs 重算 —— 由 scripts/lab/gen_eviction.py 生成，勿手改\n")
        f.write("# dev_miss_M: prompt - cached_tokens_total —— 【设备侧】没接住的部分\n")
        f.write("# host_hit_M: dev_miss - uncached —— 设备没接住、但也没重算的那部分 = host 梯队接住的。\n#             无 HiCache 时精确为 0（五条全是），有 HiCache 时 13~19M\n")
        f.write("# uncached_M: steps.jsonl 的 server_uncached_prompt_tokens 累加（真正重新 prefill 的量）\n")
        f.write("# evicted_M: metrics.csv 的 sglang:evicted_tokens_total 末值\n")
        f.write("# host_hit_M 是最关键的一列：无 HiCache 的五条精确为 0.0，有 HiCache 的八条 13~19M。\n")
        f.write("# evicted_M 单列出来只作参照——驱逐量本身不决定重算量，dev_miss 才决定。\n")
        w = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=HEADER)
        f.write("# prompt_tok/reuse_tok/uncached_tok: 未舍入的原始计数，算比例用这三列\n")
        w.writeheader()
        w.writerows(rows)
    print("写入 %s：%d 行" % (out_path, len(rows)))
    for r in rows:
        print("  %-13s hc=%s 设备未命中 %5.1fM host命中 %5.1fM 重算 %5.1fM 驱逐 %5.1fM" % (
            r["run"], r["hicache"], r["dev_miss_M"], r["host_hit_M"],
            r["uncached_M"], r["evicted_M"]))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

def direct(n):
 p=Path('results/night/pois200_'+n+'/steps.jsonl')
 if not p.exists():return None
 s=[json.loads(x) for x in open(p)]
 ts=[x['submit_timestamp'] for x in s if x.get('submit_timestamp')];te=[x['complete_timestamp'] for x in s if x.get('complete_timestamp')]
 ft=[x['first_token_ms'] for x in s if x.get('first_token_ms') is not None]
 return (max(te)-min(ts))/60,100*sum(x<=10000 for x in ft)/len(ft),len(s)

   148	
   149	Before the routes, one measurement that reframes all of them. Our controller
   150	keeps every session's permit for its lifetime, so its pool occupancy is a
   151	policy output, not a constraint. It runs at a median of 49.2\% of the pool on
   152	the base configuration, and the median number of sessions queueing for
   153	admission is 66. The configuration that finishes fastest---the repeat of that same static cap
   154	of four, at 209.1 minutes---runs at 71.9\%, and its first run at 70.2\%. Occupancy
   155	and running requests track each other reliably across the arms we have (the two
   156	columns in \texttt{occupancy.csv} rank the twenty arms almost identically), but
   157	running count does \emph{not} order wall clock: \texttt{horizon}=2 runs 1.25
   158	requests against the base arm's 1.14 and finishes 15 minutes slower. That gap is
   159	what the next subsection is about.
   160	
   161	The controller believes it is nearly full. Its own decision log records the
   162	projection it admits against---the largest of measured occupancy and the summed
   163	contexts of live sessions, plus a reserve for their growth---at a median of
   164	89{,}073 tokens against a budget of 96{,}360, i.e.\ 7\% free. Its measured
   165	occupancy at the same instants is 49{,}875 tokens: 48.2\% of that budget, or
   166	50.8\% of the pool, is idle. The
   167	projection is 1.79$\times$ the truth on this configuration. Across the
   168	controller runs we have projection logs for, the ratio runs from 1.24 to 2.00
   169	with a median of 1.75 on this trace, and from 1.24 to 4.11 with a median of
   170	1.75 once the second trace's controller arm is included (\texttt{projection.csv};
   171	the second trace is the 4.11). So 1.79 is this arm's value, not a constant of
   172	the design, and we name the set because the range depends on it---over every
   173	run in that file, including the flash-crowd family and the 25-session tier, it
   174	is 1.01 to 4.11 with a median of 1.35. Two terms produce the gap: charging every
   175	live session its full context, when a parked session's KV is evictable and
   176	therefore absent from the engine's count, and the growth reserve, which alone
   177	is 26{,}762 tokens, 28\% of the budget.
   178	
   179	Two things follow, and we are careful about the second.
   180	
   181	The first is that the controllers under-use the pool, and that this is a
   182	property of their estimates rather than of the hardware: the base
   183	configuration holds nearly half the pool idle (48.2\% of its budget, 50.8\% of\
   184	the pool) with 66 sessions queued, because
   185	it believes it has 7\% free when it has 48\%. Any account of why it is slow
   186	that does not mention this is incomplete.
   187
