    10	  上界 = sum(prefix_len) / sum(prefix_len + input_len)，从 trace 文件直接求和，
    11	         独立于任何观测（runner 的 summary.json 也报同一个数，本脚本断言二者相符）
    12	  实测复用 = prompt - uncached（steps.jsonl 的 server_uncached_prompt_tokens 累加）
    13	  兑现率 = 实测复用 / 上界 —— 「兑现了多少」这个数
    14	
    15	用法: python3 gen_reuse_x.py <trace.csv> <results_dir> <out.csv> <run> [<run> ...]
    16	"""
    17	import csv, json, os, statistics, sys
    18	
    19	
    20	def trace_bound(path):
    21	    rows = list(csv.DictReader(open(path)))
    22	    pre = sum(int(r["prefix_len"]) for r in rows)
    23	    tot = sum(int(r["prefix_len"]) + int(r["input_len"]) for r in rows)
    24	    return pre, tot, pre / tot, len({r["session_id"] for r in rows}), len(rows)
    25	
    26	
    27	def main():
    28	    trace, root, out_p = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], sys.argv[3]
    29	    runs = sys.argv[4:]
    30	    pre, tot, bound, nsess, nsteps = trace_bound(trace)
    31	
    32	    rows, bad = [], []
    33	    for r in runs:
    34	        d = os.path.join(root, r)
    35	        sp, mp, sj = (os.path.join(d, f) for f in ("steps.jsonl", "metrics.csv", "summary.json"))
    36	        if not (os.path.exists(sp) and os.path.exists(sj)):
    37	            bad.append((r, "缺 steps.jsonl 或 summary.json"))
    38	            continue
    39	        steps = [json.loads(l) for l in open(sp) if l.strip()]
    40	        prompt = sum(s.get("prompt_len") or 0 for s in steps)
    41	        unc = sum(s.get("server_uncached_prompt_tokens") or 0 for s in steps)
    42	        reuse = prompt - unc
    43	
    44	        # 断言 1：本脚本从 trace 求和得到的上界，必须等于 runner 自己报的
    45	        # planned_prefix_hit_rate —— 两个独立来源，对不上说明口径搞错了
    46	        sj_d = json.load(open(sj)).get("replay", {})
    47	        if "planned_prefix_hit_rate" in sj_d:
    48	            assert abs(sj_d["planned_prefix_hit_rate"] - bound) < 1e-4, \
    49	                "%s: 本脚本算的上界 %.4f != runner 报的 %.4f" % (r, bound, sj_d["planned_prefix_hit_rate"])
    50	        # 断言 2：实测复用不得超过上界
    51	        assert reuse <= pre, "%s: 复用 %d 超过上界 %d" % (r, reuse, pre)
    52	
    53	        rn = [float(x["sglang:num_running_reqs"]) for x in csv.DictReader(open(mp))
    54	              if x.get("sglang:num_running_reqs")]
    55	        occ = [float(x["sglang:token_usage"]) for x in csv.DictReader(open(mp))
    56	               if x.get("sglang:token_usage")]
    57	        busy = [v for v in rn if v > 0]
    58	        rows.append({
    59	            "run": r,
    60	            "sessions": nsess, "steps": len(steps),
    61	            "Rbar_busy": "%.3f" % (statistics.mean(busy) if busy else 0),
    62	            "occ_p50_pct": "%.1f" % (statistics.median(occ) * 100 if occ else 0),
    63	            "prompt_tok": prompt,
    64	            "reuse_tok": reuse,
    65	            "recompute_tok": unc,
    66	            "reuse_frac": "%.4f" % (reuse / prompt) if prompt else "",
    67	            "bound_frac": "%.4f" % bound,
    68	            "realized_frac": "%.4f" % (reuse / pre),
    69	        })
    70	
    71	    rows.sort(key=lambda x: float(x["Rbar_busy"]))
    72	    header = "\n".join([
    73	        "# 跨 trace 的复用兑现 —— 由 scripts/lab/gen_reuse_x.py 生成，勿手改",
    74	        "# trace: %s（%d 会话 %d 步）" % (os.path.basename(trace), nsess, nsteps),
    75	        "# 上界 = %d / %d = %.4f，从 trace 的 prefix_len 求和，独立于观测；" % (pre, tot, bound),
    76	        "#   与 runner summary.json 的 planned_prefix_hit_rate 断言相符。",
    77	        "# 实测复用 = prompt - uncached（steps.jsonl），兑现率 = 实测 / 上界。",
    78	        "# 用途：§VI 在第二条 trace 上重算同一组量。",

    83	        "run,sessions,steps,Rbar_busy,occ_p50_pct,prompt_tok,reuse_tok,recompute_tok,reuse_frac,bound_frac,realized_frac"])

    86	    body = header + "\n" + "\n".join(",".join(str(r[k]) for k in fields) for r in rows) + "\n"

    90

    95	    if bad:
    43

    49	        # 不归一化会静默全部落空（2026-09-24 犯过一次）。

    52	        assert len(conc) > 50, "concurrency.csv 只读到 %d 行" % len(conc)
    53

    62	            continue
    63	
    64	        prompt = int(e["prompt_tok"])
    65	        reuse = int(e["reuse_tok"])
    66	        unc = int(e["uncached_tok"])
    67	        # 用精确列，不用 0.1M 取整列 —— 同表的教训（算比例必须走未取整值）
    68	        dev_miss = int(e["dev_miss_tok"])
    69	        host_hit = int(e["host_hit_tok"])
    70	
    71	        # 断言 1：设备未命中 = host 接住 + 真正重算（这套记账的定义式；
    72	        # 挡住的是从 eviction.csv 抄错列——抄错不会有这个性质）
    73	        assert dev_miss == host_hit + unc, \

    76	        # 断言 2：复用 + 重算 = 总 prompt

    79	        # 断言 3：实测复用不得超过 trace 上界 —— 这是本表的核心不等式，

    91	            "recompute_tok": unc,
    92	            "reuse_frac": "%.4f" % (reuse / prompt),                 # 占总 prompt
    93	            # bound_frac 必须作为【数据列】出现，不能只写在注释里 —— 审计脚本
    94	            # 会跳过 # 开头的行，于是正文里的 75.5% 在数据文件里找不到出处
    95	            # （2026-09-24 审计报了 9 处假阳性才发现）。与 reuse_x.csv 的列名一致。
    96	            "bound_frac": "%.4f" % ce["ceiling_frac"],
    97	            # 上界的【原始 token 数】也要落成列：正文引用了 21,540,371 与 28,526,871，
    98	            # 而它们原来只在注释里（审计跳过注释行）。
    99	            "bound_tok": ce["ceiling_tok"],
   100	            "prompt_total_tok": ce["prompt_tok"],
   101	            "realized_frac": "%.4f" % (reuse / ce["ceiling_tok"]),   # 占 trace 上界 <<< 关键列
   102	            "tau1_ms": d["step_back_ms"],
   103	            "contested": conc.get(run, "?"),
   104	        })
   105	
    66	    spec.loader.exec_module(m)
    67	    return m
    68	
    69	
    70	def emit_csv(runs, env, delta, front_slo, conc, out_p):
    71	    """把表 I 的每一列落成一个 CSV —— 包括 Δ。
    72	
    73	    为什么必须落盘：论文声称「每个判决都由一个脚本产出」，而 Δ 是【渲染时算的】，
    74	    不存任何数据文件。审计于是把正文里每一个 Δ 数字都报成"找不到源头"
    75	    （2026-09-24 一类假阳性有十几个）。判决列必须和其它列一样可被引用。
    76	    """
    77	    FR = _frontier_mod()
    78	    fields = ["run", "label", "wall_min", "conc", "slo_pct", "front_slo",
    79	              "delta_pp", "verdict", "contested", "anchor"]
    80	    out = ["# 表 I 的每一列（含 Δ）—— 由 scripts/lab/gen_table.py 生成，勿手改",
    81	           "# front_slo = frontier.py 的 front_slo(wall)（同一函数，不是另写一遍）",
    82	           "# delta_pp  = slo_pct - front_slo；超出锚点范围为空",
    83	           "# verdict   = 按 ±1.0pp 判带：>+1 越过 / 在带内平局 / <-1 在线下",
    84	           "# contested = 同机争用窗口标记（concurrency.csv），与表里的 ‡ 同源",
    85	           ",".join(fields)]
   148	        f = front_slo(float(r["wall_min"]))
   149	        return None if f is None else float(r["slo_pct"]) - f
   150	
   151	    def row(k, anchor=False):
   152	        r = runs[k]
   153	        d = delta(k)
   154	        ds = "\\emph{anchor}" if anchor else ("$+$%.2f" % d if d > 0 else "$-$%.2f" % -d)
   155	        if anchor:
   156	            ds = "\\emph{anchor}"
   157	        # 显示回 1 位小数（表里就是这样印的），但 ds 是用全精度算出来的
   158	        return "    %-17s & %.1f & %s & %.1f & %s \\\\" % (
   159	            LABEL[k] + dagger(k), float(r["wall_min"]), r["conc"],
   160	            float(r["slo_pct"]), ds)
   161	
   162	    print("    \\multicolumn{5}{l}{\\emph{Static caps}}\\\\")
   163	    for k in ["default", "cap1", "cap2", "cap3_fixed", "cap4", "cap5"]:
   164	        print(row(k, anchor=(k == "cap1")))
    22	RESULTS = "/data/xbw/turnstile/results/night"
    23	# 上左包络的锚点，按墙钟升序。cap1 没有 HiCache 版本，但它仍然是"静态 cap 能达到的点"，
    24	# 必须放进前沿——否则墙钟超过最右锚点的 run 会被误判成"越过前沿"（cap1 自己就撞过这个）。
    25	FRONT = [(209.1, 68.5, "cap4_hc_r2"), (230.3, 81.9, "cap3_hc_r2"),
    26	         (295.0, 91.6, "cap2_hc"), (527.4, 94.8, "cap1")]
    27	
    28	
    29	def front_slo(wall):
    30	    """前沿在给定墙钟处的 SLO 上界（分段线性插值）。超出锚点范围返回 None——
    31	    范围外没有可比的前沿，不能给判决，否则端点外推会凭空造出"越过"。"""
    32	    if wall < FRONT[0][0] or wall > FRONT[-1][0]:
    33	        return None
    34	    for (w0, s0, _), (w1, s1, _) in zip(FRONT, FRONT[1:]):
    35	        if w0 <= wall <= w1:
    36	            return s0 + (s1 - s0) * (wall - w0) / (w1 - w0)
    37	    return None
    38

codex
I recomputed the requested quantities from the replay CSVs, step logs, metric samples, and paper data files. **The main numerical problems are inconsistent prompt counters in the reuse calculation, slopes calculated from rounded inputs, and Table I distances interpolated from rounded anchors.**

| Item | Verdict | Recomputed result |
|---|---|---|
| **A — trace bound** | **MATCH** | `21,540,371 / 28,526,871 = 75.5091%`, or **75.5%**. [sec_model.tex](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_model.tex:227) · [reuse.csv](/root/paper/foyer/paper/data/reuse.csv:18) |
| **A — main-trace realization** | **MISMATCH** | Using each run’s `steps.jsonl` prompt and uncached counts consistently gives device-only **92.5% → 36.4%**, host-tier static **90.2–92.4%**, and all ten host-tier runs **84.2–92.4%**. The paper prints **92.6 → 36.3**, **90.2–92.3**, and **83.9–92.3**. Its generator subtracts step-log uncached tokens from the *sampled metric counter* for prompts; that counter differs from the step-log prompt sum. [sec_model.tex](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_model.tex:252) · [reuse.csv](/root/paper/foyer/paper/data/reuse.csv:19) · [gen_eviction.py](/root/paper/foyer/scripts/lab/gen_eviction.py:115) |
| **A — recomputation** | **MATCH** | **8,592,024 → 20,695,653 tokens**, displayed as **8.6M → 20.7M**. [sec_model.tex](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_model.tex:252) · [reuse.csv](/root/paper/foyer/paper/data/reuse.csv:19) |
| **A — realhr realization** | **MATCH** | **74.779, 72.150, 67.496, 64.032%** at the displayed running counts **1.076, 1.160, 1.287, 1.409**. These print as **74.8, 72.2, 67.5, 64.0%**. [sec_xtrace.tex](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_xtrace.tex:30) · [reuse_x.csv](/root/paper/foyer/paper/data/reuse_x.csv:12) |
| **A — slopes** | **MISMATCH** | From step-log reuse counts and unrounded running means in `metrics.csv`, the main whole-range slope is **−66.8** as printed, but its segments are **−10.4, −40.6, −157.1, −127.6**, versus **−10.8, −41.0, −157.4, −126.7**. Realhr is **−32.3** whole-range, with segments **−31.2, −36.8, −28.4**, versus **−31.3, −36.6, −28.4**. Even using `reuse.csv`’s token counts and its rounded running means, the second main segment is **−40.9**, not **−41.0**. [sec_xtrace.tex](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_xtrace.tex:35) · [slope.csv](/root/paper/foyer/paper/data/slope.csv:8) · [gen_slope.py](/root/paper/foyer/scripts/lab/gen_slope.py:20) |
| **B — tool waits** | **MISMATCH** | The medians (**20.195 s / 0.338 s**) and means (**39.865 s / 15.822 s**) match their displayed values. The median ratio is **59.7×**, but the mean ratio is **2.52×**. “Roughly 60× either way” is false for the means. [sec_xtrace.tex](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_xtrace.tex:20) · [toolwait.csv](/root/paper/foyer/paper/data/toolwait.csv:8) |
| **B — round-1+ hit rates** | **MATCH** | All five device-only rates, three host-tier rates, controller **80.6%**, and planned **80.9%** agree with step-log means at the stated precision. [sec_discussion.tex](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_discussion.tex:152) · [hitrate.csv](/root/paper/foyer/paper/data/hitrate.csv:5) |
| **B — idle stretches** | **MATCH** | **1,849** stretches; mean **20.648 s → 20.6 s**; **69.610% → 69.6%** of idle time and **17.631% → 17.6%** of stretches exceed 20 s. Pooling changes the median from **5.0–5.6 s** to **10.0 s**, supporting the stated lack of robustness. [sec_model.tex](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_model.tex:20) · [idle_pooled.csv](/root/paper/foyer/paper/data/idle_pooled.csv:6) |
| **C — Table I Δ** | **MISMATCH** | The printed entries reproduce `table1.csv`’s `delta_pp` to two decimals. **Recomputing from that same file’s more precise anchor coordinates does not.** For example, single-flight off is **−0.55 pp**, not **−0.57**; cap4 is **−29.17**, not **−29.19**. The three numeric anchor distances become **0.00**. `frontier.py` hard-codes anchors rounded to one decimal before interpolation. The verdict bands do not change. [sec_results.tex](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_results.tex:113) · [table1.csv](/root/paper/foyer/paper/data/table1.csv:16) · [frontier.py](/root/paper/foyer/scripts/lab/frontier.py:25) |
| **C — τ ranges and gaps** | **MATCH** | Across the stated 13 runs, raw recomputation gives τ₁ **26.974–36.696 ms**, τ₂ **20.641–33.114 ms**, gap **2.557–8.193 ms**, and excess **7.723–39.690%**. Each matches the paper’s precision. [sec_model.tex](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_model.tex:104) · [sec_model.tex](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_model.tex:143) · [decomp.csv](/root/paper/foyer/paper/data/decomp.csv:6) |
| **D — concurrency counts** | **MATCH** | `concurrency.csv` has **14 CONTESTED** runs, **six** Table I rows, four affected controller rows at **30.9–33.3 ms**, and `cap1`/published AIMD at **20.6/24.4 ms**. [sec_discussion.tex](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_discussion.tex:341) · [concurrency.csv](/root/paper/foyer/paper/data/concurrency.csv:97) · [concurrency.csv](/root/paper/foyer/paper/data/concurrency.csv:120) |

## Major issues

1. **The main-trace reuse quantities combine different prompt sources.** For example, `cap3_fixed` has **28,483,759** prompt tokens in `reuse.csv` but **28,526,871** across its completed step records. This changes realization from the paper’s **78.9%** to **79.0%**. The realhr generator uses prompt and uncached counts from the same step records; the main generator does not. Rebuild main reuse and slopes using one definition. [reuse.csv](/root/paper/foyer/paper/data/reuse.csv:21) · [gen_reuse_x.py](/root/paper/foyer/scripts/lab/gen_reuse_x.py:39)

2. **Derived slopes and Table I distances still use rounded inputs.** The slope generator reads four-decimal realization fractions and three-decimal running means; the frontier interpolates between one-decimal anchors. Both fail the project’s stated precision rule despite having generated CSV outputs. [gen_slope.py](/root/paper/foyer/scripts/lab/gen_slope.py:20) · [frontier.py](/root/paper/foyer/scripts/lab/frontier.py:25)

3. **“Roughly 60× either way” should be corrected:** it is about **60× at the median** and **2.5× at the mean**. [sec_xtrace.tex](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_xtrace.tex:20)

## Minor issues

- The paper calls **92.6%** a “ceiling-on-prompt”; the observed prompt reuse for `cap1` is about **69.9%**. The roughly 92.5–92.6% figure uses the *trace reuse bound* as denominator. [sec_model.tex](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_model.tex:262) · [reuse.csv](/root/paper/foyer/paper/data/reuse.csv:19)
- **89,073 of 96,360 tokens** leaves **7.56%** free, which rounds to **7.6%** or **8%**, rather than the paper’s **7%**. [sec_results.tex](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_results.tex:161) · [projection.csv](/root/paper/foyer/paper/data/projection.csv:15)
- Four `table1.csv` labels contain unquoted commas, giving those rows **11 fields under a 10-field header**. This impedes ordinary CSV parsing, though their displayed Δ values can still be recovered. [table1.csv](/root/paper/foyer/paper/data/table1.csv:17) · [table1.csv](/root/paper/foyer/paper/data/table1.csv:29)

## Acceptance assessment

**Roughly 15–30% for CCF-B / CAS Q2 if submitted in this state**, as a numerical-integrity judgement rather than a venue prediction. The central direction of the negative result survives these recomputations, and the Table I verdicts appear unchanged. The range rests on the conspicuous mean-ratio error, inconsistent reuse accounting, remaining rounded-input calculations, and the four flagged controller rows the paper itself treats as unusable pending remeasurement.
